     1→---
     2→title: CI/CD Pipeline
     3→sidebar_label: CI/CD Pipeline
     4→sidebar_position: 8
     5→description: GitHub Actions pipeline stages, security gates, and scanning
     6→---
     7→
     8→# CI/CD Pipeline
     9→
    10→This document covers the GitHub Actions CI/CD pipeline architecture, stages, and security gates.
    11→
    12→## Pipeline Architecture
    13→
    14→Every service repo follows the **same pipeline** by calling reusable workflows from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows`.
    15→
    16→### Shared Workflows Repository
    17→
    18→```
    19→kix-platform-ci-workflows/
    20→└── .github/workflows/
    21→    ├── standard-pipeline.yaml      # Main orchestrator
    22→    ├── _lint-and-test.yaml         # Language-aware lint + unit tests
    23→    ├── _secret-scan.yaml           # Trivy filesystem secret detection
    24→    ├── _sca.yaml                   # Dependency-Track SCA analysis
    25→    ├── _sast.yaml                  # SonarQube static analysis
    26→    ├── _build-image.yaml           # Docker build + BuildKit cache
    27→    ├── _container-scan-sign.yaml   # Trivy image scan + SBOM + Cosign
    28→    ├── _push-registry.yaml         # Push to Artifact Registry
    29→    ├── _update-gitops.yaml         # Dispatch to gitops repo
    30→    ├── _promote.yaml               # Promote between environments
    31→    └── _dast.yaml                  # OWASP ZAP baseline scan
    32→```
    33→
    34→### Service Repo CI Workflow
    35→
    36→Every service copies this minimal workflow file:
    37→
    38→```yaml
    39→# .github/workflows/ci.yaml
    40→name: CI
    41→on:
    42→  push:
    43→    branches: [main]
    44→  pull_request:
    45→    branches: [main]
    46→  workflow_dispatch:
    47→
    48→jobs:
    49→  pipeline:
    50→    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1
    51→    with:
    52→      service-name: ${{ github.event.repository.name }}
    53→      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile
    54→      chart-path: ./chart
    55→      python-version: "3.11"     # or node-version: "20"
    56→    secrets: inherit
    57→```
    58→
    59→## Pipeline Stages
    60→
    61→### Code Pipeline (Every PR and Merge)
    62→
    63→```
    64→┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
    65→│  1. LINT &   │──│  2. SECRET   │──│  3. SCA      │──│  4. SAST     │──│  5. BUILD &  │
    66→│     TEST     │  │     SCAN     │  │              │  │              │  │  UNIT TEST   │
    67→└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘
    68→  PR + merge        PR + merge        PR + merge        PR + merge        merge only
    69→  • ruff/eslint     • Trivy fs        • Syft SBOM       • SonarQube       • BuildKit
    70→  • type checks     • --scanners      • generation      • analysis        • multi-stage
    71→  • pip audit/        secret          • Dependency-     • quality gate:   • unit tests
    72→    npm audit       • stops on          Track upload     no critical/    • OCI labels
    73→                      any finding     • stops on           high issues
    74→                                        critical/high
    75→
    76→┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐
    77→│  6. CONTAINER│──│  7. PUSH &   │──│  8. DEPLOY   │
    78→│  SCAN & SIGN │  │  REGISTRY    │  │  (GitOps)    │
    79→└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘
    80→  merge only        merge only        merge only
    81→  • Trivy image     • Push to AR      • Dispatch to
    82→  • scan            • Tag: sha-xxx    • gitops repo
    83→  • SBOM attach     • Tag: v1.2.3     • Update dev
    84→  • (Syft)            (on git tag)    • values.yaml
    85→  • Cosign sign                       • ArgoCD syncs
    86→    (OIDC)
    87→```
    88→
    89→### When Stages Run
    90→
    91→| Stage | On PR | On Merge | Purpose |
    92→|-------|-------|----------|---------|
    93→| 1. Lint & Test | ✓ | ✓ | Fast feedback on code quality |
    94→| 2. Secret Scan | ✓ | ✓ | Block secrets before commit |
    95→| 3. SCA | ✓ | ✓ | Dependency vulnerability check |
    96→| 4. SAST | ✓ | ✓ | Static code analysis |
    97→| 5. Build & Test | ✗ | ✓ | Docker image build |
    98→| 6. Container Scan | ✗ | ✓ | Image vulnerability scan + sign |
    99→| 7. Push | ✗ | ✓ | Push to Artifact Registry |
   100→| 8. Deploy | ✗ | ✓ | GitOps update for dev |
   101→
   102→## Security Gates
   103→
   104→All gates are **fail-stop** — pipeline halts immediately on critical/high findings.
   105→
   106→### Gate Details
   107→
   108→| Gate | Tool | Stage | Trigger | Action on Failure |
   109→|------|------|-------|---------|-------------------|
   110→| **Secret Detection** | Trivy (`--scanners secret`) | 2 | Every PR + merge | Stop pipeline, no exceptions |
   111→| **Dependency Vulnerabilities** | Dependency-Track (via Syft SBOM) | 3 | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |
   112→| **SAST Quality Gate** | SonarQube | 4 | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |
   113→| **Container Vulnerabilities** | Trivy (image scan) | 6 | Every merge | Stop on critical/high |
   114→| **Image Signature** | Cosign (Sigstore/OIDC) | 6 | Every merge | Unsigned images blocked |
   115→| **DAST Baseline** | OWASP ZAP | 9 (post-deploy) | Before prod promotion | Block promotion on findings |
   116→| **Admission Control** | Kyverno | Deploy-time | Every sync | Reject unsigned images |
   117→
   118→### Tool Configuration
   119→
   120→#### Trivy Secret Scan
   121→
   122→Scans source filesystem before any build:
   123→
   124→```yaml
   125→- name: Secret Scan
   126→  run: |
   127→    trivy fs --scanners secret --exit-code 1 .
   128→```
   129→
   130→Zero tolerance — any finding stops the pipeline.
   131→
   132→#### SonarQube
   133→
   134→Hosted in `shared-devsecops` namespace:
   135→
   136→- Runs static analysis on every PR
   137→- Quality gate: no new critical or high severity issues
   138→- Results posted as PR comment
   139→
   140→#### Dependency-Track
   141→
   142→SBOM-based vulnerability tracking:
   143→
   144→- Syft generates CycloneDX SBOMs
   145→- Uploaded to Dependency-Track for continuous monitoring
   146→- Pipeline stops on critical/high vulnerabilities
   147→
   148→#### Cosign Image Signing
   149→
   150→Keyless signing via GitHub OIDC → Sigstore:
   151→
   152→```yaml
   153→- name: Sign Image
   154→  run: |
   155→    cosign sign --yes $IMAGE_REF
   156→```
   157→
   158→No long-lived keys — fully automatic.
   159→
   160→### Vulnerability Remediation SLAs
   161→
   162→Per the Konecta DevSecOps Handbook:
   163→
   164→| Severity | Detection → Triage | Triage → Fix | Fix → Deploy |
   165→|----------|-------------------|--------------|--------------|
   166→| Critical | 4 hours | 24 hours | 48 hours |
   167→| High | 24 hours | 7 days | 14 days |
   168→| Medium | 7 days | 30 days | 45 days |
   169→| Low | 30 days | 90 days | Next release |
   170→
   171→## Image Tagging Strategy
   172→
   173→| Tag | When | Purpose |
   174→|-----|------|---------|
   175→| `sha-{7-char}` | Every build | Immutable build identifier |
   176→| `v{major}.{minor}.{patch}` | On git tag | Release version |
   177→| `{env}-latest` | On promotion | Environment pointer (mutable) |
   178→
   179→**Rule**: SHA tags are immutable. Never overwrite. Promotion copies the SHA tag reference, not the image.
   180→
   181→## Secrets Management (CI)
   182→
   183→### GitHub OIDC → GCP Workload Identity
   184→
   185→No JSON keys — fully automated authentication:
   186→
   187→```yaml
   188→- name: Authenticate to GCP
   189→  uses: google-github-actions/auth@v2
   190→  with:
   191→    workload_identity_provider: ${{ secrets.WIF_PROVIDER }}
   192→    service_account: ${{ secrets.WIF_SA }}
   193→```
   194→
   195→| Property | Value |
   196→|----------|-------|
   197→| Token lifetime | ~1 hour |
   198→| Auto-refreshing | Yes |
   199→| Audit trail | Full via Cloud Audit Logs |
   200→
   201→### Build Secrets
   202→
   203→For build-time secrets (never in image layers):
   204→
   205→```dockerfile
   206→RUN --mount=type=secret,id=npm_token \
   207→    NPM_TOKEN=$(cat /run/secrets/npm_token) npm ci
   208→```
   209→
   210→```yaml
   211→- name: Build
   212→  run: |
   213→    docker build --secret id=npm_token,src=${{ secrets.NPM_TOKEN }} .
   214→```
   215→
   216→### Cross-Org Dispatch
   217→
   218→GitHub App installed across all orgs enables cross-repo dispatch:
   219→
   220→- Minimal permissions (write to gitops repo only)
   221→- Scoped to `kix-platform-infrastructure` commits
   222→
   223→## Example Pipeline Run
   224→
   225→```
   226→1. Developer pushes to feature branch
   227→2. PR opened to main
   228→3. Pipeline runs stages 1-4 (lint, secret scan, SCA, SAST)
   229→4. PR review and approval
   230→5. Merge to main
   231→6. Pipeline runs all stages 1-8
   232→7. Image built, scanned, signed, pushed
   233→8. GitOps repo updated with new image tag
   234→9. ArgoCD syncs to dev environment
   235→10. Service deployed and running
   236→```
   237→
   238→## Required Repository Configuration
   239→
   240→### Variables (Settings → Actions → Variables)
   241→
   242→| Variable | Example |
   243→|----------|---------|
   244→| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | `805102662749-...` (for Node.js builds) |
   245→| `ENVIRONMENT` | `development` / `staging` / `production` |
   246→
   247→### Secrets (Inherited from Org)
   248→
   249→| Secret | Description |
   250→|--------|-------------|
   251→| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` | Workload Identity pool provider |
   252→| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` | Service account for CI |
   253→
   254→### Branch → Environment Mapping
   255→
   256→| Branch | Environment | Namespace |
   257→|--------|-------------|-----------|
   258→| `develop` | dev | `dev-api-services` |
   259→| `staging` | qa | `qa-api-services` |
   260→| `main` | prod | `prod-api-services` |
   261→
   262→---
   263→
   264→**Next**: [GitOps & Promotion](gitops-promotion) — ArgoCD GitOps flow and environment promotion.
   265→
```

     1→---
     2→title: GitOps & Promotion
     3→sidebar_label: GitOps & Promotion
     4→sidebar_position: 9
     5→description: ArgoCD GitOps flow, environment promotion, and rollback procedures
     6→---
     7→
     8→# GitOps & Promotion
     9→
    10→This document covers the ArgoCD GitOps flow, environment promotion strategy, and rollback procedures.
    11→
    12→## GitOps Flow
    13→
    14→### Cross-Repository Flow
    15→
    16→```
    17→konecta-ix-mcp-servers/                    konecta-ix-platform/
    18→kix-mcp-gateway                            kix-platform-infrastructure
    19→────────────────────                       ───────────────────────────
    20→1. PR merged to main
    21→2. Pipeline builds sha-abc1234
    22→3. Image pushed to Artifact Registry
    23→4. Pipeline triggers repository_dispatch ──▶ 5. Automated PR or direct commit:
    24→                                                environments/dev/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
    25→                                                  image.tag: sha-abc1234
    26→                                             6. ArgoCD detects change → syncs dev
    27→```
    28→
    29→### GitOps Repository Structure
    30→
    31→```
    32→kix-platform-infrastructure/
    33→├── argocd/
    34→│   └── applicationset.yaml          # Matrix generator: env × service
    35→└── environments/
    36→    ├── dev/
    37→    │   └── api-services/
    38→    │       └── kix-mcp-gateway.yaml # Dev overrides
    39→    ├── qa/
    40→    │   └── api-services/
    41→    │       └── kix-mcp-gateway.yaml # QA overrides
    42→    └── prod/
    43→        └── api-services/
    44→            └── kix-mcp-gateway.yaml # Prod overrides
    45→```
    46→
    47→### Environment Values File
    48→
    49→Each service has a values file per environment:
    50→
    51→```yaml
    52→# environments/dev/api-services/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
    53→base-service-chart:
    54→  image:
    55→    tag: "sha-abc1234"  # Updated by CI pipeline
    56→
    57→  containers:
    58→    app:
    59→      env:
    60→        NODE_ENV: "development"
    61→        LOG_LEVEL: "debug"
    62→
    63→  autoscaling:
    64→    minReplicas: 1
    65→    maxReplicas: 2
    66→```
    67→
    68→## ArgoCD Configuration
    69→
    70→### ApplicationSet with Git Directory Generator
    71→
    72→ArgoCD auto-discovers services from the GitOps repo structure:
    73→
    74→```yaml
    75→apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
    76→kind: ApplicationSet
    77→metadata:
    78→  name: kix-services
    79→  namespace: shared-argocd
    80→spec:
    81→  generators:
    82→    - git:
    83→        repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure
    84→        revision: main
    85→        directories:
    86→          - path: environments/*/api-services/*
    87→          - path: environments/*/mcp-servers/*
    88→          - path: environments/*/web-apps/*
    89→  template:
    90→    metadata:
    91→      name: '{{path.basename}}-{{path[1]}}'
    92→    spec:
    93→      project: '{{path[1]}}-project'
    94→      source:
    95→        repoURL: '# extracted from values file'
    96→        path: chart
    97→        helm:
    98→          valueFiles:
    99→            - '{{path}}'
   100→      destination:
   101→        server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
   102→        namespace: '{{path[1]}}-{{path[2]}}'
   103→      syncPolicy:
   104→        automated:
   105→          prune: true
   106→          selfHeal: true
   107→```
   108→
   109→### Sync Policies by Environment
   110→
   111→| Environment | Sync Mode | When |
   112→|-------------|-----------|------|
   113→| **Dev** | `automated` (auto-sync) | 24/7 |
   114→| **QA** | `automated: false` (manual) | 08:00-18:00 UTC |
   115→| **Prod** | `automated: false` (manual) | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC |
   116→
   117→### ArgoCD Projects
   118→
   119→RBAC isolation per environment:
   120→
   121→```yaml
   122→apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
   123→kind: AppProject
   124→metadata:
   125→  name: dev-project
   126→  namespace: shared-argocd
   127→spec:
   128→  description: Development environment
   129→  sourceRepos:
   130→    - 'https://github.com/konecta-ix-*'
   131→  destinations:
   132→    - namespace: 'dev-*'
   133→      server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
   134→  clusterResourceWhitelist:
   135→    - group: ''
   136→      kind: Namespace
   137→```
   138→
   139→## Environment Promotion
   140→
   141→### Promotion Workflow
   142→
   143→The `_promote.yaml` reusable workflow handles promotion:
   144→
   145→```yaml
   146→# Triggered manually in service repo
   147→name: Promote
   148→on:
   149→  workflow_dispatch:
   150→    inputs:
   151→      from_env:
   152→        description: Source environment
   153→        required: true
   154→        type: choice
   155→        options: [dev, qa]
   156→      to_env:
   157→        description: Target environment
   158→        required: true
   159→        type: choice
   160→        options: [qa, prod]
   161→```
   162→
   163→### Dev → QA Promotion
   164→
   165→1. Engineer triggers `promote` workflow in service repo
   166→2. Workflow copies image tag from `environments/dev/{service}.yaml` to `environments/qa/{service}.yaml`
   167→3. Creates a PR on kix-platform-infrastructure
   168→4. After merge, ArgoCD syncs to QA
   169→
   170→### QA → Prod Promotion (with DAST)
   171→
   172→1. Engineer triggers `promote` workflow targeting prod
   173→2. Workflow runs **OWASP ZAP baseline scan** against QA endpoint
   174→3. If ZAP finds vulnerabilities → promotion blocked, findings reported
   175→4. If ZAP passes → workflow copies image tag to `environments/prod/{service}.yaml`
   176→5. Creates a PR on kix-platform-infrastructure (requires approval)
   177→6. After merge, ArgoCD syncs to prod
   178→
   179→### Change Windows
   180→
   181→| Environment | Window | Notes |
   182→|-------------|--------|-------|
   183→| **Dev** | 24/7 | Auto-approve allowed |
   184→| **QA** | 08:00-18:00 UTC (Mon-Fri) | Manual approval required |
   185→| **Prod** | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC | Manual approval + change ticket |
   186→
   187→## Rollback Procedures
   188→
   189→### Automatic Rollback (Prod)
   190→
   191→Prometheus alert on error rate > 5% for 3 minutes:
   192→
   193→1. Alert fires
   194→2. Webhook triggers rollback job
   195→3. Job reverts commit in kix-platform-infrastructure
   196→4. ArgoCD syncs previous image tag
   197→
   198→**RTO target**: < 5 minutes (detection + revert + sync)
   199→
   200→### Manual Rollback via Git
   201→
   202→Fastest rollback method:
   203→
   204→```bash
   205→cd kix-platform-infrastructure
   206→
   207→# Revert the last commit
   208→git revert HEAD
   209→git push
   210→
   211→# ArgoCD auto-syncs (dev) or manual sync (qa/prod)
   212→```
   213→
   214→### Manual Rollback via kubectl
   215→
   216→For emergency when git is unavailable:
   217→
   218→```bash
   219→# Roll back to previous ReplicaSet
   220→kubectl rollout undo deployment/kix-mcp-gateway -n dev-mcp-servers
   221→
   222→# Verify
   223→kubectl rollout status deployment/kix-mcp-gateway -n dev-mcp-servers
   224→```
   225→
   226→:::warning
   227→kubectl rollback doesn't update Git — reconcile Git state after emergency.
   228→:::
   229→
   230→### ArgoCD Rollback
   231→
   232→Via ArgoCD UI or CLI:
   233→
   234→```bash
   235→# List recent syncs
   236→argocd app history kix-mcp-gateway-dev
   237→
   238→# Rollback to specific revision
   239→argocd app rollback kix-mcp-gateway-dev <revision>
   240→```
   241→
   242→## Sync Waves
   243→
   244→Resources deploy in order via sync waves:
   245→
   246→| Wave | Resources |
   247→|------|-----------|
   248→| 0 | Namespaces, RBAC, SecretStores |
   249→| 5 | ConfigMaps, ExternalSecrets |
   250→| 10 | Deployments, Services, HPA |
   251→| 15 | Ingress, NetworkPolicies |
   252→
   253→Example annotation:
   254→
   255→```yaml
   256→metadata:
   257→  annotations:
   258→    argocd.argoproj.io/sync-wave: "10"
   259→```
   260→
   261→## Health Checks
   262→
   263→ArgoCD monitors resource health:
   264→
   265→| Resource | Health Check |
   266→|----------|--------------|
   267→| Deployment | All replicas ready |
   268→| Service | Endpoints exist |
   269→| Ingress | Address assigned |
   270→| HPA | Current replicas within bounds |
   271→
   272→### Custom Health Check
   273→
   274→For ExternalSecrets:
   275→
   276→```lua
   277→hs = {}
   278→if obj.status ~= nil then
   279→  if obj.status.conditions ~= nil then
   280→    for i, condition in ipairs(obj.status.conditions) do
   281→      if condition.type == "Ready" and condition.status == "True" then
   282→        hs.status = "Healthy"
   283→        return hs
   284→      end
   285→    end
   286→  end
   287→end
   288→hs.status = "Progressing"
   289→return hs
   290→```
   291→
   292→## Notifications
   293→
   294→ArgoCD notifications on sync events:
   295→
   296→| Event | Channel | Recipients |
   297→|-------|---------|------------|
   298→| Sync success | Slack #deployments | Team channel |
   299→| Sync failure | Slack #deployments + PagerDuty | Team + On-call |
   300→| Health degraded | Slack #alerts | Team channel |
   301→| Prod deployment | Slack #prod-changes | Wider audience |
   302→
   303→### Example Notification Template
   304→
   305→```yaml
   306→apiVersion: v1
   307→kind: ConfigMap
   308→metadata:
   309→  name: argocd-notifications-cm
   310→data:
   311→  template.app-sync-succeeded: |
   312→    message: |
   313→      Application {{.app.metadata.name}} sync succeeded.
   314→      Revision: {{.app.status.sync.revision}}
   315→      Environment: {{.app.spec.destination.namespace}}
   316→```
   317→
   318→## Disaster Recovery
   319→
   320→### Multi-Region Failover
   321→
   322→1. Secondary GKE cluster in eu-west4 (pre-provisioned)
   323→2. DNS-based failover to secondary cluster
   324→3. ArgoCD can deploy to secondary from same Git state
   325→
   326→### Recovery Steps
   327→
   328→1. Detect primary failure (health check timeout)
   329→2. Update DNS to point to secondary LB
   330→3. ArgoCD in secondary syncs from gitops repo
   331→4. Services running on secondary within 15 minutes
   332→
   333→### State Recovery
   334→
   335→Application state in managed services (Cloud SQL, GCS) — replicated separately.
   336→
   337→---
   338→
   339→**Next**: [Backstage & Onboarding](backstage-onboarding) — Developer portal and service onboarding.
   340→
```

     1→---
     2→title: Security Architecture
     3→sidebar_label: Security
     4→sidebar_position: 11
     5→description: Runtime security, secrets management, and admission control for migrated services
     6→---
     7→
     8→# Security Architecture
     9→
    10→This document covers runtime security, secrets management, and admission control for migrated services.
    11→
    12→## Container Security
    13→
    14→### Image Security
    15→
    16→| Control | Implementation |
    17→|---------|----------------|
    18→| **Multi-stage builds** | Builder dependencies never ship in runtime |
    19→| **Base image policy** | Pinned digests, updated monthly, slim/distroless only |
    20→| **Vulnerability scanning** | Trivy pre-push, CRITICAL/HIGH block deployment |
    21→| **Image signing** | Cosign keyless (OIDC) — every image cryptographically signed |
    22→| **SBOM** | Generated for every build, stored as OCI annotation |
    23→| **Registry** | Artifact Registry with vulnerability scanning, IAM-restricted |
    24→
    25→### Supply Chain Security
    26→
    27→| Control | Implementation |
    28→|---------|----------------|
    29→| **Dependency pinning** | Hash-verified `requirements.txt`, `package-lock.json` |
    30→| **Dependency scanning** | `pip audit` / `npm audit` in CI |
    31→| **Automated updates** | Renovate for dependency PRs |
    32→| **GitHub Actions hardening** | Pin actions by SHA, minimal GITHUB_TOKEN permissions |
    33→| **No third-party actions** | Without security review |
    34→| **OIDC authentication** | No stored credentials for GCP interactions |
    35→
    36→## Runtime Security
    37→
    38→### Pod Security Standards
    39→
    40→All pods run with `restricted` profile:
    41→
    42→```yaml
    43→podSecurityContext:
    44→  runAsNonRoot: true
    45→  runAsUser: 65532
    46→  fsGroup: 65532
    47→  seccompProfile:
    48→    type: RuntimeDefault
    49→
    50→containerSecurityContext:
    51→  allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
    52→  readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
    53→  capabilities:
    54→    drop: [ALL]
    55→```
    56→
    57→### Restrictions
    58→
    59→| Setting | Value | Rationale |
    60→|---------|-------|-----------|
    61→| `runAsNonRoot` | `true` | No root processes |
    62→| `readOnlyRootFilesystem` | `true` | Prevent filesystem modifications |
    63→| `allowPrivilegeEscalation` | `false` | No privilege escalation |
    64→| `capabilities.drop` | `[ALL]` | No Linux capabilities |
    65→| `hostNetwork` | `false` | No host network access |
    66→| `hostPID` | `false` | No host PID namespace |
    67→| `hostIPC` | `false` | No host IPC namespace |
    68→| `privileged` | `false` | No privileged containers |
    69→
    70→### Writable Paths
    71→
    72→Only explicit `emptyDir` mounts are writable:
    73→
    74→```yaml
    75→volumeMounts:
    76→  - name: tmp
    77→    mountPath: /tmp
    78→  - name: cache
    79→    mountPath: /app/.cache
    80→
    81→volumes:
    82→  - name: tmp
    83→    emptyDir:
    84→      sizeLimit: 100Mi
    85→  - name: cache
    86→    emptyDir:
    87→      sizeLimit: 500Mi
    88→```
    89→
    90→## Secrets Management
    91→
    92→### External Secrets Operator (ESO)
    93→
    94→ESO syncs secrets from GCP Secret Manager to Kubernetes:
    95→
    96→```
    97→GCP Secret Manager → ESO SecretStore → ExternalSecret → K8s Secret → Pod
    98→```
    99→
   100→### SecretStore Configuration
   101→
   102→Per-namespace SecretStore authenticated via Workload Identity:
   103→
   104→```yaml
   105→apiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1
   106→kind: SecretStore
   107→metadata:
   108→  name: gcp-secret-store
   109→  namespace: dev-api-services
   110→spec:
   111→  provider:
   112→    gcpsm:
   113→      projectID: kd-ix-platform
   114→      auth:
   115→        workloadIdentity:
   116→          clusterLocation: europe-west1
   117→          clusterName: kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
   118→          serviceAccountRef:
   119→            name: external-secrets-sa
   120→```
   121→
   122→### ExternalSecret Example
   123→
   124→```yaml
   125→apiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1
   126→kind: ExternalSecret
   127→metadata:
   128→  name: kix-mcp-gateway-secrets
   129→  namespace: dev-mcp-servers
   130→spec:
   131→  refreshInterval: 1h
   132→  secretStoreRef:
   133→    name: gcp-secret-store
   134→    kind: SecretStore
   135→  target:
   136→    name: kix-mcp-gateway-secrets
   137→    creationPolicy: Owner
   138→  data:
   139→    - secretKey: DB_PASSWORD
   140→      remoteRef:
   141→        key: kix-mcp-gateway-db-password
   142→    - secretKey: API_KEY
   143→      remoteRef:
   144→        key: kix-mcp-gateway-api-key
   145→```
   146→
   147→### Secret Rotation
   148→
   149→- ESO refresh interval: 1 hour
   150→- Secrets auto-updated without pod restart (if using mounted secrets)
   151→- For environment variables: rolling restart via deployment annotation
   152→
   153→### What NOT to Store in Secrets
   154→
   155→| Data | Where to Store |
   156→|------|----------------|
   157→| Configuration (non-sensitive) | ConfigMaps |
   158→| Feature flags | ConfigMaps or external service |
   159→| Large files | GCS with signed URLs |
   160→| Certificates | cert-manager (auto-managed) |
   161→
   162→## Workload Identity
   163→
   164→### No Service Account Keys
   165→
   166→Every service uses Workload Identity for GCP API access:
   167→
   168→```yaml
   169→serviceAccount:
   170→  annotations:
   171→    iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account: kix-mcp-gateway@kd-ix-platform.iam.gserviceaccount.com
   172→```
   173→
   174→### IAM Binding
   175→
   176→```hcl
   177→resource "google_service_account_iam_binding" "workload_identity" {
   178→  service_account_id = google_service_account.sa.name
   179→  role               = "roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser"
   180→  members = [
   181→    "serviceAccount:${var.project_id}.svc.id.goog[${var.namespace}/${var.k8s_sa}]"
   182→  ]
   183→}
   184→```
   185→
   186→### Least Privilege Roles
   187→
   188→| Service Type | Required Roles |
   189→|--------------|----------------|
   190→| **API with Cloud SQL** | `roles/cloudsql.client` |
   191→| **With GCS access** | `roles/storage.objectViewer` or `objectAdmin` |
   192→| **With Vertex AI** | `roles/aiplatform.user` |
   193→| **With Pub/Sub** | `roles/pubsub.publisher` or `subscriber` |
   194→| **With Secret Manager** | `roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor` |
   195→
   196→## Network Security
   197→
   198→### Default Deny
   199→
   200→All application namespaces have default deny ingress:
   201→
   202→```yaml
   203→apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
   204→kind: NetworkPolicy
   205→metadata:
   206→  name: default-deny-ingress
   207→spec:
   208→  podSelector: {}
   209→  policyTypes:
   210→    - Ingress
   211→```
   212→
   213→### Explicit Allow Rules
   214→
   215→Services explicitly allow traffic from:
   216→
   217→- GCP health check IPs (35.191.0.0/16, 130.211.0.0/22)
   218→- Kong namespace (for API services)
   219→- Gateway namespace (for frontend services)
   220→- Specific internal services as needed
   221→
   222→### Private Cluster
   223→
   224→| Setting | Value |
   225→|---------|-------|
   226→| Public node IPs | Disabled |
   227→| Master authorised networks | VPN/bastion only |
   228→| Private Google Access | Enabled |
   229→| Egress | Via Cloud NAT |
   230→
   231→## Admission Control
   232→
   233→### Kyverno Policies
   234→
   235→Kyverno enforces security policies at admission time:
   236→
   237→| Policy | Effect |
   238→|--------|--------|
   239→| `require-image-signature` | Reject unsigned images (prod) |
   240→| `disallow-latest-tag` | Block `:latest` tag |
   241→| `require-resource-limits` | Enforce CPU/memory limits |
   242→| `require-konecta-registry` | Only allow Artifact Registry images |
   243→| `restrict-capabilities` | Enforce capability drop |
   244→| `require-labels` | Enforce standard labels |
   245→
   246→### Example Policy: Require Image Signature
   247→
   248→```yaml
   249→apiVersion: kyverno.io/v1
   250→kind: ClusterPolicy
   251→metadata:
   252→  name: require-image-signature
   253→spec:
   254→  validationFailureAction: enforce
   255→  rules:
   256→    - name: verify-signature
   257→      match:
   258→        resources:
   259→          kinds:
   260→            - Pod
   261→          namespaces:
   262→            - "prod-*"
   263→      verifyImages:
   264→        - imageReferences:
   265→            - "europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/*"
   266→          attestors:
   267→            - entries:
   268→                - keyless:
   269→                    subject: "https://github.com/konecta-ix-*"
   270→                    issuer: "https://token.actions.githubusercontent.com"
   271→```
   272→
   273→### Example Policy: Block Latest Tag
   274→
   275→```yaml
   276→apiVersion: kyverno.io/v1
   277→kind: ClusterPolicy
   278→metadata:
   279→  name: disallow-latest-tag
   280→spec:
   281→  validationFailureAction: enforce
   282→  rules:
   283→    - name: validate-image-tag
   284→      match:
   285→        resources:
   286→          kinds:
   287→            - Pod
   288→      validate:
   289→        message: "Using ':latest' tag is not allowed."
   290→        pattern:
   291→          spec:
   292→            containers:
   293→              - image: "!*:latest"
   294→```
   295→
   296→## RBAC
   297→
   298→### Namespace Isolation
   299→
   300→Each namespace has dedicated:
   301→
   302→- ServiceAccount per application
   303→- Role with minimal permissions
   304→- RoleBinding to ServiceAccount
   305→
   306→### ArgoCD Project RBAC
   307→
   308→```yaml
   309→apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
   310→kind: AppProject
   311→metadata:
   312→  name: prod-project
   313→spec:
   314→  destinations:
   315→    - namespace: 'prod-*'
   316→      server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
   317→  sourceRepos:
   318→    - 'https://github.com/konecta-ix-*'
   319→  roles:
   320→    - name: deployer
   321→      policies:
   322→        - p, proj:prod-project:deployer, applications, sync, prod-project/*, allow
   323→      groups:
   324→        - platform-team
   325→```
   326→
   327→## Audit Logging
   328→
   329→### GCP Audit Logs
   330→
   331→All GCP API calls logged:
   332→
   333→- Admin Activity (always on)
   334→- Data Access (enabled for sensitive services)
   335→- System Events
   336→
   337→### Kubernetes Audit Logs
   338→
   339→Cluster audit logs sent to Cloud Logging:
   340→
   341→- RequestReceived
   342→- ResponseStarted
   343→- ResponseComplete
   344→
   345→### Log Retention
   346→
   347→| Log Type | Hot Storage | Archive |
   348→|----------|-------------|---------|
   349→| Application logs | 30 days | 7 years |
   350→| Audit logs | 400 days | 7 years |
   351→| Security events | 1 year | 7 years |
   352→
   353→## Compliance
   354→
   355→### EU Data Residency
   356→
   357→All data and processing in EU regions only:
   358→
   359→```hcl
   360→variable "region" {
   361→  validation {
   362→    condition     = can(regex("^europe-", var.region))
   363→    error_message = "Region must be in Europe"
   364→  }
   365→}
   366→```
   367→
   368→### Allowed Regions
   369→
   370→- `europe-west1` (Belgium)
   371→- `europe-west4` (Netherlands)
   372→
   373→### Organisation Policy Constraints
   374→
   375→Enforced at org level:
   376→
   377→| Constraint | Effect |
   378→|------------|--------|
   379→| No public IPs on VMs | Enforced |
   380→| Domain-restricted IAM | `konecta-group.com` only |
   381→| No service account key downloads | Enforced |
   382→| Audit logging mandatory | Enforced |
   383→| EU regions only | Enforced |
   384→
   385→---
   386→
   387→**Next**: [Observability](observability) — Monitoring, logging, and alerting.
   388→
```

     1→---
     2→title: Kubernetes & Helm
     3→sidebar_label: Kubernetes & Helm
     4→sidebar_position: 5
     5→description: Helm chart strategy, namespace design, resource profiles, and autoscaling
     6→---
     7→
     8→# Kubernetes & Helm
     9→
    10→This document covers Helm chart design, namespace strategy, resource profiles, health probes, and autoscaling configuration.
    11→
    12→## Helm Chart Strategy
    13→
    14→### Three-Layer Architecture
    15→
    16→The platform uses a three-layer Helm architecture matching the multi-org, repo-per-service model:
    17→
    18→```
    19→Layer 1: Base Chart Library (platform org)
    20→         └── kix-platform-modules/helm/base-service-chart/
    21→             ├── Chart.yaml (type: library)
    22→             └── templates/_*.tpl
    23→
    24→Layer 2: Service Chart (each service repo)
    25→         └── kix-mcp-gateway/chart/
    26→             ├── Chart.yaml (depends on base chart)
    27→             └── values.yaml (service defaults)
    28→
    29→Layer 3: Environment Values (GitOps repo)
    30→         └── kix-platform-infrastructure/environments/
    31→             ├── dev/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
    32→             ├── qa/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
    33→             └── prod/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml
    34→```
    35→
    36→### Base Chart Library
    37→
    38→Published to Artifact Registry as an OCI Helm chart. Contains templated resources:
    39→
    40→| Template | Purpose |
    41→|----------|---------|
    42→| `_deployment.tpl` | Pod spec, containers, sidecars |
    43→| `_service.tpl` | K8s Service definitions |
    44→| `_ingress.tpl` | Kong Ingress / HTTPRoute |
    45→| `_hpa.tpl` | HorizontalPodAutoscaler |
    46→| `_pdb.tpl` | PodDisruptionBudget |
    47→| `_networkpolicy.tpl` | Network isolation |
    48→| `_serviceaccount.tpl` | Workload Identity |
    49→| `_external-secret.tpl` | External Secrets CRD |
    50→
    51→### Service Chart
    52→
    53→Each service chart depends on the base chart and provides service-specific defaults:
    54→
    55→```yaml
    56→# chart/Chart.yaml
    57→apiVersion: v2
    58→name: kix-mcp-gateway
    59→version: 0.1.0
    60→type: application
    61→
    62→dependencies:
    63→  - name: base-service-chart
    64→    version: "~1.0.0"
    65→    repository: "oci://europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/helm-charts"
    66→```
    67→
    68→### Base Chart Versioning
    69→
    70→- Published to Artifact Registry as OCI Helm chart
    71→- Service charts pin to a specific version (`~1.0.0`)
    72→- Renovate automates version bumps across all orgs
    73→- Breaking changes require major version bump
    74→
    75→## Namespace Strategy
    76→
    77→### Environment × Domain Boundaries (16 Namespaces)
    78→
    79→| Namespace Pattern | Contents |
    80→|-------------------|----------|
    81→| `{env}-api-services` | FastAPI backends, business apps, bots, data pipelines |
    82→| `{env}-mcp-servers` | MCP protocol servers |
    83→| `{env}-web-apps` | Nuxt 3 frontends |
    84→| `shared-kong` | Kong API Gateway |
    85→| `shared-litellm` | LiteLLM proxy (Kong-only access) |
    86→| `shared-argocd` | ArgoCD (existing) |
    87→| `shared-backstage` | Backstage developer portal |
    88→| `shared-monitoring` | Prometheus, Grafana, Jaeger |
    89→| `shared-devsecops` | SonarQube, Dependency-Track |
    90→| `shared-cert-manager` | TLS certificate management |
    91→
    92→### Namespace Configuration
    93→
    94→Each namespace receives:
    95→
    96→- **ResourceQuotas**: CPU/memory limits per namespace
    97→- **LimitRanges**: Default container limits
    98→- **NetworkPolicies**: Default deny-all ingress
    99→- **RBAC bindings**: Team-scoped access
   100→
   101→## Resource Profiles
   102→
   103→### Standard Profiles
   104→
   105→| Profile | CPU Request | CPU Limit | Mem Request | Mem Limit | Use Case |
   106→|---------|-------------|-----------|-------------|-----------|----------|
   107→| **API Service** | 100m | 500m | 256Mi | 512Mi | FastAPI APIs, MCP servers, bots, data pipelines |
   108→| **API Heavy** | 250m | 1000m | 512Mi | 1Gi | RAG ingestion, document processing, ML dependencies |
   109→| **Frontend** | 50m | 200m | 128Mi | 256Mi | Nuxt 3 SSR |
   110→
   111→### Cloud SQL Proxy Sidecar
   112→
   113→When using Cloud SQL Auth Proxy:
   114→
   115→```yaml
   116→resources:
   117→  requests:
   118→    cpu: 50m
   119→    memory: 64Mi
   120→  limits:
   121→    cpu: 200m
   122→    memory: 128Mi
   123→```
   124→
   125→## Health Probes
   126→
   127→### Probe Configuration by Service Type
   128→
   129→| Service Type | Liveness | Readiness | Startup |
   130→|--------------|----------|-----------|---------|
   131→| **FastAPI** | `GET /healthz` :8000 | `GET /readyz` :8000 (checks DB) | N/A |
   132→| **MCP Server (SSE)** | `GET /healthz` :8000, period=30s | `GET /readyz` :8000, initial=30s | N/A |
   133→| **Nuxt 3** | `GET /` :3000 | `GET /_health` :3000 | N/A |
   134→| **Heavy services** | `GET /healthz` :8000 | `GET /readyz` :8000 | failureThreshold=18 (3min) |
   135→
   136→### Example Probe Configuration
   137→
   138→```yaml
   139→probes:
   140→  liveness:
   141→    httpGet:
   142→      path: /healthz
   143→      port: 8080
   144→    initialDelaySeconds: 30
   145→    periodSeconds: 15
   146→    failureThreshold: 3
   147→  readiness:
   148→    httpGet:
   149→      path: /readyz
   150→      port: 8080
   151→    initialDelaySeconds: 40
   152→    periodSeconds: 10
   153→    failureThreshold: 3
   154→  startup:
   155→    httpGet:
   156→      path: /healthz
   157→      port: 8080
   158→    initialDelaySeconds: 10
   159→    periodSeconds: 5
   160→    failureThreshold: 12  # 60s max startup
   161→```
   162→
   163→## Autoscaling (HPA)
   164→
   165→### HPA Configuration by Tier
   166→
   167→| Tier | Min | Max | Target CPU | Scale-Down Window |
   168→|------|-----|-----|------------|-------------------|
   169→| **User-facing** (frontends, public APIs) | 2 | 20 | 60% | 5 min |
   170→| **Internal APIs, MCP servers** | 2 | 10 | 75% | 10 min |
   171→| **Data pipelines, bots** | 1 | 5 | 80% | 15 min |
   172→
   173→### Custom Metrics
   174→
   175→Via Prometheus Adapter:
   176→
   177→- Request rate
   178→- Pub/Sub queue depth
   179→- Active SSE connections
   180→
   181→### Example HPA Values
   182→
   183→```yaml
   184→autoscaling:
   185→  enabled: true
   186→  minReplicas: 2
   187→  maxReplicas: 10
   188→  targetCPUUtilization: 70
   189→  targetMemoryUtilization: 80
   190→```
   191→
   192→## Cloud SQL Auth Proxy Sidecar
   193→
   194→Services connecting to Cloud SQL use the **Cloud SQL Auth Proxy** as a sidecar container.
   195→
   196→### Why Sidecar (Not Shared Proxy)
   197→
   198→| Benefit | Description |
   199→|---------|-------------|
   200→| **Per-pod authentication** | Workload Identity per service account |
   201→| **Least-privilege IAM** | Each service has minimum required permissions |
   202→| **No bottleneck** | Scales with application pods |
   203→| **Google-recommended** | Official pattern for GKE |
   204→
   205→### How It Works
   206→
   207→```
   208→┌────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   209→│  Pod                                       │
   210→│  ┌──────────────────┐  ┌────────────────┐  │
   211→│  │  App Container   │  │  cloud-sql-    │  │
   212→│  │  (FastAPI)       │──│  proxy sidecar │────────▶ Cloud SQL
   213→│  │  :8000           │  │  :5432 (TCP)   │  │
   214→│  └──────────────────┘  └────────────────┘  │
   215→│  localhost:5432                            │
   216→└────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   217→```
   218→
   219→### Helm Values Configuration
   220→
   221→```yaml
   222→cloudSqlProxy:
   223→  enabled: true
   224→  instanceConnectionName: "project:region:instance"
   225→  port: 5432
   226→  resources:
   227→    requests:
   228→      cpu: 50m
   229→      memory: 64Mi
   230→    limits:
   231→      cpu: 200m
   232→      memory: 128Mi
   233→```
   234→
   235→### Connection String Pattern
   236→
   237→```
   238→DATABASE_URL=postgresql+asyncpg://user@localhost:5432/dbname
   239→```
   240→
   241→No password needed when using `--auto-iam-authn` with Workload Identity.
   242→
   243→## Multi-Tier App Pattern
   244→
   245→Two-tiered apps (Nuxt frontend + FastAPI backend) deploy as **multi-container pods**.
   246→
   247→### Pod Layout
   248→
   249→```
   250→┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   251→│  Pod: kix-app-hrfraud                                            │
   252→│                                                                  │
   253→│  ┌──────────────────┐   localhost:8000   ┌────────────────────┐  │
   254→│  │  frontend        │ ─────────────────▶ │  backend           │  │
   255→│  │  (Nuxt SSR)      │                    │  (FastAPI)         │  │
   256→│  │  :3000           │                    │  :8000             │  │
   257→│  └──────────────────┘                    └─────────┬──────────┘  │
   258→│                                                    │ localhost   │
   259→│                                          ┌─────────▼──────────┐  │
   260→│                                          │  cloud-sql-proxy   │  │
   261→│                                          │  :5432             │───▶ Cloud SQL
   262→│                                          └────────────────────┘  │
   263→└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   264→         ▲                    ▲
   265→         │                    │
   266→    Frontend LB          API LB (Kong)
   267→  (Gateway API + IAP)   (internal routes)
   268→```
   269→
   270→### Benefits
   271→
   272→| Benefit | Description |
   273→|---------|-------------|
   274→| **localhost communication** | Zero network hop between frontend and backend |
   275→| **Atomic deploys** | Both containers deploy together, no version skew |
   276→| **Shared lifecycle** | Same release cycle, same Helm chart |
   277→| **Simpler networking** | No inter-service DNS, K8s Service only for external access |
   278→| **Fewer resources** | One Deployment, one HPA, one PDB |
   279→
   280→### When NOT to Use
   281→
   282→- Services needing independent scaling
   283→- Very different resource profiles
   284→- Loosely coupled services with different release cadences
   285→- Static SPA frontends (serve from CDN instead)
   286→
   287→### Helm Values Example
   288→
   289→```yaml
   290→containers:
   291→  backend:
   292→    image:
   293→      repository: eu-docker.pkg.dev/project/kix-services/kix-app-hrfraud-backend
   294→      tag: sha-abc1234
   295→    port: 8000
   296→    env:
   297→      DATABASE_URL: "postgresql+asyncpg://user@localhost:5432/dbname"
   298→      LITELLM_ENDPOINT: "http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/llm"
   299→
   300→  frontend:
   301→    image:
   302→      repository: eu-docker.pkg.dev/project/kix-services/kix-app-hrfraud-frontend
   303→      tag: sha-def5678
   304→    port: 3000
   305→    env:
   306→      API_BASE: "http://localhost:8000"
   307→
   308→cloudSqlProxy:
   309→  enabled: true
   310→  instanceConnectionName: "project:eu-west1:hrfraud-db"
   311→
   312→services:
   313→  api:
   314→    port: 8000
   315→    ingress:
   316→      class: kong
   317→      path: /api/hrfraud
   318→  frontend:
   319→    port: 3000
   320→    ingress:
   321→      class: gateway-api
   322→      host: hrfraud.app.konecta.io
   323→```
   324→
   325→## ArgoCD ApplicationSet Strategy
   326→
   327→### Git Directory Generator
   328→
   329→ArgoCD watches `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure`. Each file in `environments/{env}/` defines an application.
   330→
   331→| Environment | Sync Policy | Change Window |
   332→|-------------|-------------|---------------|
   333→| **Dev** | `automated` (auto-sync) | 24/7 |
   334→| **QA** | `automated: false` (manual) | 08:00-18:00 UTC |
   335→| **Prod** | `automated: false` (manual) | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC |
   336→
   337→### ArgoCD Projects
   338→
   339→- `dev-project` — RBAC for dev namespace deployments
   340→- `qa-project` — RBAC for qa namespace deployments
   341→- `prod-project` — RBAC for prod namespace deployments
   342→
   343→### Sync Waves
   344→
   345→- **Wave 0**: Infrastructure resources (namespaces, secrets, configmaps)
   346→- **Wave 10**: Application deployments
   347→
   348→---
   349→
   350→**Next**: [Ingress & Networking](ingress-networking) — Dual ingress architecture and network policies.
   351→
```

     1→---
     2→title: Application Migration
     3→sidebar_label: Overview
     4→sidebar_position: 1
     5→description: Migration guide for moving Cloud Run services to the KIX-Infrastructure Kubernetes platform
     6→---
     7→
     8→# Application Migration
     9→
    10→This section provides a comprehensive guide for migrating services from **GCP Cloud Run** to the **kix-infrastructure** Kubernetes platform. The migration adopts modern cloud-native patterns including Helm charts, GitOps-driven deployments, and a unified CI/CD pipeline.
    11→
    12→## Executive Summary
    13→
    14→The platform is migrating approximately **24 microservices** from Cloud Run to a Kubernetes-native architecture. This migration delivers:
    15→
    16→- **Standardised packaging** with Helm charts for declarative Kubernetes deployment
    17→- **Secure container builds** with multi-stage Docker, vulnerability scanning, and image signing
    18→- **Unified CI/CD** via GitHub Actions for build/scan/push and ArgoCD for GitOps deployments
    19→- **Developer portal** via Backstage for service catalog, scaffolding, and documentation
    20→- **Dual ingress architecture** with Kong for APIs and GKE Gateway API + IAP for frontends
    21→
    22→**Key principles**: Simple, secure, auditable, repeatable.
    23→
    24→## Migration Scope
    25→
    26→### In Scope (24 Services)
    27→
    28→| Category | Count | Stack |
    29→|----------|-------|-------|
    30→| MCP Servers | 14 | Python (FastMCP) |
    31→| Business Apps | 4 | Python (FastAPI) + Node.js (Nuxt 3) |
    32→| Data Pipelines | 3 | Python |
    33→| Bots | 1 | Python |
    34→| Prototypes | 2 | Python |
    35→
    36→### Out of Scope
    37→
    38→- **CrewAI Flows & Tools** (12) — remain on current platform
    39→- **Infrastructure Repos** (4) — handled separately
    40→- **Config/Proxies** (2) — remain as-is
    41→
    42→### External Dependencies (Remain as Managed Services)
    43→
    44→- Cloud SQL (PostgreSQL 16+)
    45→- Firestore
    46→- Vertex AI (Gemini, Claude via Vertex, RAG Engine)
    47→- Cloud Storage (GCS)
    48→- Pub/Sub
    49→- Google APIs (Search, Maps, Drive, Chat)
    50→
    51→## Documentation Structure
    52→
    53→| Section | Description |
    54→|---------|-------------|
    55→| [Current State](current-state) | Cloud Run architecture, service inventory, and dependencies |
    56→| [Target Architecture](target-architecture) | GKE target architecture, repository model, and CI/CD flow |
    57→| [Migration Phases](migration-phases) | Phased migration strategy with priorities and timeline |
    58→| [Kubernetes & Helm](kubernetes-helm) | Helm chart design, namespaces, resource profiles, and autoscaling |
    59→| [Ingress & Networking](ingress-networking) | Dual ingress architecture, network policies, and load balancing |
    60→| [Docker Security](docker-security) | Secure multi-stage builds, base images, and hardening |
    61→| [CI/CD Pipeline](cicd-pipeline) | GitHub Actions pipeline stages and security gates |
    62→| [GitOps & Promotion](gitops-promotion) | ArgoCD GitOps flow, environment promotion, and rollback |
    63→| [Backstage & Onboarding](backstage-onboarding) | Developer portal integration and service onboarding |
    64→| [Security Architecture](security) | Runtime security, secrets management, and admission control |
    65→| [Observability](observability) | Monitoring, logging, tracing, and alerting |
    66→| [Operations & Risks](operations-risks) | Cost considerations, disaster recovery, and risk register |
    67→| [Appendix & Reference](appendix-reference) | Technology stack, namespace map, and repository mapping |
    68→
    69→## Service Migration Guides
    70→
    71→Detailed step-by-step guides for migrating specific services:
    72→
    73→| Service | Status | Guide |
    74→|---------|--------|-------|
    75→| IQ Portal | P0 (Pilot) | [IQ Portal Migration Guide](service-guides/iq-portal) |
    76→
    77→## Quick Links
    78→
    79→- **Platform Modules**: [kix-platform-modules](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules)
    80→- **Infrastructure Repo**: [kix-platform-infrastructure](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure)
    81→- **CI Workflows**: [kix-platform-ci-workflows](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows)
    82→
    83→---
    84→
    85→**Last Updated:** February 2026
    86→**Maintained By:** Platform Engineering Team
    87→

     1→---
     2→title: Backstage & Onboarding
     3→sidebar_label: Backstage & Onboarding
     4→sidebar_position: 10
     5→description: Developer portal integration and service onboarding
     6→---
     7→
     8→# Backstage & Onboarding
     9→
    10→This document covers Backstage integration and the process for onboarding new services to the platform.
    11→
    12→## Backstage Overview
    13→
    14→**Backstage** (`shared-backstage` namespace) serves as the internal developer portal, providing a single pane of glass across all 24+ services spread across 6 GitHub organisations.
    15→
    16→### Core Capabilities
    17→
    18→| Capability | Backstage Feature | Integration |
    19→|------------|------------------|-------------|
    20→| **Service catalog** | Software Catalog | Auto-discovers `catalog-info.yaml` from all GitHub orgs |
    21→| **New service creation** | Software Templates | Pre-built templates for Python/Node.js/MCP services |
    22→| **Documentation** | TechDocs | Renders `docs/` markdown into unified portal |
    23→| **CI/CD visibility** | GitHub Actions plugin | Pipeline status, build history per service |
    24→| **Deployment status** | ArgoCD plugin | Sync status, deployed image tag, health per environment |
    25→| **Live K8s status** | Kubernetes plugin | Pod count, status, resource usage, logs |
    26→| **Security posture** | SonarQube + Dependency-Track plugins | Code quality, vulnerability counts |
    27→| **API documentation** | API docs plugin | Renders OpenAPI/Swagger specs |
    28→| **Ownership** | Catalog model | Maps services → teams → GitHub orgs |
    29→
    30→## Service Onboarding
    31→
    32→### Option A: Via Backstage Software Template (Recommended)
    33→
    34→The fastest way to create a new service:
    35→
    36→1. **Open Backstage** at `https://backstage.app.konecta.io`
    37→2. **Create Component** → Select template:
    38→   - Python API
    39→   - Node.js API
    40→   - Nuxt Frontend
    41→   - MCP Server
    42→3. **Fill in the form**:
    43→   - Service name
    44→   - Owning team
    45→   - GitHub org
    46→   - Cloud SQL requirement (yes/no)
    47→   - Ingress type (Kong/Gateway API/both)
    48→4. **Backstage scaffolds the repo** with:
    49→   - Standard `Dockerfile`
    50→   - Standard `chart/` directory
    51→   - Standard `.github/workflows/ci.yaml`
    52→   - Pre-filled `catalog-info.yaml`
    53→   - `docs/index.md` stub for TechDocs
    54→5. **Backstage creates GitOps entries**:
    55→   - `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/{service-name}.yaml` in kix-platform-infrastructure
    56→6. **ArgoCD auto-discovers** via ApplicationSet
    57→7. **First push** triggers standard pipeline
    58→8. **Service appears in Backstage catalog**
    59→
    60→### Option B: Manual Onboarding
    61→
    62→For cases where template doesn't fit:
    63→
    64→1. **Create repo** from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template`:
    65→   ```bash
    66→   gh repo create konecta-ix-mcp-servers/kix-mcp-newservice \
    67→     --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template \
    68→     --private
    69→   ```
    70→
    71→2. **Copy application code** and configure:
    72→   - Update `Dockerfile`
    73→   - Configure `chart/values.yaml`
    74→   - Create `catalog-info.yaml`
    75→
    76→3. **Add to GitOps repo**:
    77→   ```bash
    78→   # In kix-platform-infrastructure
    79→   mkdir -p environments/{dev,qa,prod}/mcp-servers
    80→   touch environments/dev/mcp-servers/kix-mcp-newservice.yaml
    81→   # Configure values and commit
    82→   ```
    83→
    84→4. **ArgoCD auto-discovers** from git directory
    85→
    86→5. **Backstage auto-discovers** `catalog-info.yaml` within 30 minutes
    87→
    88→## catalog-info.yaml
    89→
    90→Every service repo must include a `catalog-info.yaml` for Backstage discovery:
    91→
    92→```yaml
    93→apiVersion: backstage.io/v1alpha1
    94→kind: Component
    95→metadata:
    96→  name: kix-mcp-gateway
    97→  description: MCP Gateway — routes MCP protocol requests to backend servers
    98→  annotations:
    99→    # GitHub
   100→    github.com/project-slug: konecta-ix-mcp-servers/kix-mcp-gateway
   101→    # ArgoCD
   102→    argocd/app-name: kix-mcp-gateway
   103→    # Kubernetes
   104→    backstage.io/kubernetes-id: kix-mcp-gateway
   105→    backstage.io/kubernetes-namespace: dev-mcp-servers,qa-mcp-servers,prod-mcp-servers
   106→    # TechDocs
   107→    backstage.io/techdocs-ref: dir:.
   108→    # SonarQube
   109→    sonarqube.org/project-key: kix-mcp-gateway
   110→  tags:
   111→    - python
   112→    - mcp
   113→    - fastmcp
   114→  links:
   115→    - url: https://api.konecta.io/api/mcp-gateway/health
   116→      title: Health Check
   117→      icon: dashboard
   118→spec:
   119→  type: service
   120→  lifecycle: production
   121→  owner: group:mcp-integration-team
   122→  system: kix-platform
   123→  providesApis:
   124→    - mcp-gateway-api
   125→  dependsOn:
   126→    - component:kix-services-kong
   127→    - component:kix-services-litellm
   128→```
   129→
   130→### Key Annotations
   131→
   132→| Annotation | Purpose |
   133→|------------|---------|
   134→| `github.com/project-slug` | Links to GitHub repo |
   135→| `argocd/app-name` | Shows deployment status |
   136→| `backstage.io/kubernetes-id` | Shows pod status |
   137→| `backstage.io/kubernetes-namespace` | Which namespaces to query |
   138→| `backstage.io/techdocs-ref` | TechDocs source location |
   139→| `sonarqube.org/project-key` | Links to code quality |
   140→
   141→## Software Templates
   142→
   143→Backstage hosts 4 Software Templates in `kix-platform-backstage/templates/`:
   144→
   145→| Template | Creates | Includes |
   146→|----------|---------|----------|
   147→| `python-api` | Python FastAPI service | Dockerfile, chart/, ci.yaml, catalog-info.yaml, docs/ |
   148→| `nodejs-api` | Node.js Express service | Dockerfile, chart/, ci.yaml, catalog-info.yaml, docs/ |
   149→| `mcp-server` | Python FastMCP server | Dockerfile, chart/, ci.yaml, catalog-info.yaml, docs/ |
   150→| `nuxt-frontend` | Nuxt 3 frontend | Dockerfile, chart/, ci.yaml, catalog-info.yaml, docs/ |
   151→
   152→### Template Parameters
   153→
   154→Each template prompts for:
   155→
   156→| Parameter | Description |
   157→|-----------|-------------|
   158→| `name` | Service name (e.g., `kix-mcp-newservice`) |
   159→| `description` | Brief description |
   160→| `owner` | Owning team (Backstage group) |
   161→| `githubOrg` | Target GitHub organisation |
   162→| `cloudSql` | Needs Cloud SQL? (adds sidecar) |
   163→| `ingressType` | Kong, Gateway API, or both |
   164→
   165→## Catalog Discovery
   166→
   167→Backstage discovers services via GitHub organisation-wide discovery:
   168→
   169→```yaml
   170→# Backstage app-config.yaml
   171→catalog:
   172→  providers:
   173→    github:
   174→      konecta-ix-platform:
   175→        organization: konecta-ix-platform
   176→        catalogPath: /catalog-info.yaml
   177→        schedule:
   178→          frequency: { minutes: 30 }
   179→          timeout: { minutes: 3 }
   180→      konecta-ix-mcp-servers:
   181→        organization: konecta-ix-mcp-servers
   182→        catalogPath: /catalog-info.yaml
   183→        schedule:
   184→          frequency: { minutes: 30 }
   185→          timeout: { minutes: 3 }
   186→      # ... other orgs
   187→```
   188→
   189→### Discovery Frequency
   190→
   191→- Automatic refresh: every 30 minutes
   192→- Manual refresh: via Backstage UI (Catalog → Refresh)
   193→
   194→## TechDocs
   195→
   196→### Setup
   197→
   198→Every service should include a `docs/` directory and `mkdocs.yml`:
   199→
   200→```
   201→service-repo/
   202→├── docs/
   203→│   └── index.md
   204→└── mkdocs.yml
   205→```
   206→
   207→### mkdocs.yml
   208→
   209→```yaml
   210→site_name: MCP Gateway
   211→nav:
   212→  - Home: index.md
   213→  - API: api.md
   214→  - Runbook: runbook.md
   215→plugins:
   216→  - techdocs-core
   217→```
   218→
   219→### Building TechDocs
   220→
   221→TechDocs are built automatically by Backstage or can be pre-built in CI:
   222→
   223→```yaml
   224→- name: Build TechDocs
   225→  run: |
   226→    npx @techdocs/cli generate --source-dir . --output-dir ./site
   227→    npx @techdocs/cli publish --publisher-type googleGcs \
   228→      --storage-name $TECHDOCS_BUCKET \
   229→      --entity default/component/kix-mcp-gateway
   230→```
   231→
   232→## Backstage Plugins
   233→
   234→### Installed Plugins
   235→
   236→| Plugin | Purpose |
   237→|--------|---------|
   238→| `@backstage/plugin-github-actions` | CI pipeline status |
   239→| `@roadiehq/backstage-plugin-argo-cd` | Deployment status |
   240→| `@backstage/plugin-kubernetes` | Pod status, logs |
   241→| `@backstage/plugin-sonarqube` | Code quality metrics |
   242→| `@backstage/plugin-techdocs` | Documentation rendering |
   243→| `@backstage/plugin-api-docs` | OpenAPI documentation |
   244→| `@backstage/plugin-catalog-import` | Entity import |
   245→
   246→### Plugin Configuration
   247→
   248→Example ArgoCD plugin config:
   249→
   250→```yaml
   251→# app-config.yaml
   252→argocd:
   253→  baseUrl: https://argocd.konecta.io
   254→  appLocatorMethods:
   255→    - type: 'config'
   256→      instances:
   257→        - name: main
   258→          url: https://argocd.konecta.io
   259→```
   260→
   261→## Onboarding Checklist
   262→
   263→```markdown
   264→## New Service Onboarding Checklist
   265→
   266→### Repository Setup
   267→- [ ] Created from template or Backstage scaffolder
   268→- [ ] CODEOWNERS file configured
   269→- [ ] .dockerignore present
   270→- [ ] Branch protection enabled
   271→
   272→### Backstage Integration
   273→- [ ] catalog-info.yaml present and valid
   274→- [ ] docs/index.md created
   275→- [ ] mkdocs.yml configured
   276→- [ ] Service appears in Backstage catalog
   277→
   278→### CI/CD
   279→- [ ] ci.yaml calls standard pipeline
   280→- [ ] GitHub secrets/variables configured
   281→- [ ] First pipeline run successful
   282→
   283→### GitOps
   284→- [ ] Environment values files created
   285→- [ ] ArgoCD discovers the application
   286→- [ ] Dev deployment successful
   287→
   288→### Verification
   289→- [ ] Service running in dev
   290→- [ ] Health endpoints responding
   291→- [ ] Metrics visible in Grafana
   292→- [ ] TechDocs rendering in Backstage
   293→```
   294→
   295→---
   296→
   297→**Next**: [Security Architecture](security) — Runtime security and secrets management.
   298→
```

     1→---
     2→title: IQ Portal Migration Guide
     3→sidebar_label: IQ Portal
     4→sidebar_position: 2
     5→description: Complete migration guide for IQ Portal from Cloud Run to kix-infrastructure
     6→---
     7→
     8→# IQ Portal Migration Guide
     9→
    10→**Service**: kon-iq-portal → `kix-usecase-iq-portal`
    11→**Target Org**: `konecta-ix-workloads`
    12→**Phase**: P0 (Pilot)
    13→
    14→---
    15→
    16→## 1. Current State Assessment
    17→
    18→### Architecture
    19→
    20→IQ Portal is a **single-container monolith**: an Express.js (TypeScript) backend serves both the API (`/api/*`) and a pre-generated Nuxt 4 SPA (static files from `/dist`). Everything runs on port **8080** in production.
    21→
    22→```
    23→┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
    24→│  Cloud Run (kon-iq-portal)              │
    25→│                                         │
    26→│  Express.js :8080                       │
    27→│  ├── /api/*  → API routes               │
    28→│  ├── /docs   → Swagger UI               │
    29→│  └── /*      → Static Nuxt SPA          │
    30→│                                         │
    31→│  Cloud SQL (Unix socket)                │
    32→│  /cloudsql/project:region:instance      │
    33→└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
    34→```
    35→
    36→### Current Tech Stack
    37→
    38→| Component | Detail |
    39→|-----------|--------|
    40→| Runtime | Node.js 22 (Alpine) |
    41→| Frontend | Nuxt 4 (SPA, `ssr: false`, pre-generated static) |
    42→| Backend | Express 5 (TypeScript, via `tsx`) |
    43→| Database | PostgreSQL on Cloud SQL (`kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform`) |
    44→| DB Connection | Unix socket via VPC connector (`DB_HOST=/cloudsql/...`) |
    45→| Migrations | Custom SQL/TS runner (33 migrations, runs on startup) |
    46→| Auth | Google OAuth → JWT in HttpOnly cookies |
    47→| Storage | GCS (3 buckets: images, videos, RAG docs) |
    48→| AI | Vertex AI (Gemini) + RAG corpus |
    49→| Async Jobs | Cloud Tasks → kon-rag-ingestion |
    50→| Email | ChannelHub (external Cognito-auth API) |
    51→| CI/CD | Cloud Build from Bitbucket |
    52→| Port | 8080 (production), 3002 (dev) |
    53→
    54→### GCP Service Dependencies
    55→
    56→| Service | Project | Used For |
    57→|---------|---------|----------|
    58→| Cloud SQL (PostgreSQL) | `kd-lab-464110` | Primary database |
    59→| Cloud Storage | `kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev` | Image/video/RAG file storage |
    60→| Vertex AI | `kd-lab-464110` | Gemini LLM + RAG retrieval |
    61→| Cloud Tasks | `kd-lab-464110` | Async RAG document ingestion |
    62→| Secret Manager | `kd-lab-464110` | JWT secret, DB password, RAG token |
    63→
    64→---
    65→
    66→## 2. Target State
    67→
    68→```
    69→┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
    70→│  kix-infrastructure GKE Cluster                                     │
    71→│                                                                     │
    72→│  Namespace: dev-api-services (then qa-api-services, prod-...)       │
    73→│                                                                     │
    74→│  ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
    75→│  │  Pod: kix-usecase-iq-portal                                   │  │
    76→│  │                                                               │  │
    77→│  │  ┌──────────────────────┐   ┌──────────────────────────────┐  │  │
    78→│  │  │  app (Express)       │   │  cloud-sql-proxy sidecar     │  │  │
    79→│  │  │  :8080               │──▶│  :5432 (TCP)                 │──────▶ Cloud SQL
    80→│  │  │  /api/* + SPA        │   │  --private-ip                │  │  │
    81→│  │  └──────────────────────┘   └──────────────────────────────┘  │  │
    82→│  └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
    83→│      │                                                              │
    84→│      ▼                                                              │
    85→│  Service: kix-usecase-iq-portal :8080                               │
    86→│      │                         │                                    │
    87→│      ▼                         ▼                                    │
    88→│  Kong Ingress              GKE Gateway API                          │
    89→│  /api/iq-portal/*          iq-portal.app.konecta.io                 │
    90→│  (API consumers)           (browser users via IAP)                  │
    91→└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
    92→```
    93→
    94→### Key Differences from Cloud Run
    95→
    96→| Aspect | Cloud Run | kix-infrastructure |
    97→|--------|-----------|-------------------|
    98→| DB connection | Unix socket via VPC connector | Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (TCP `localhost:5432`) |
    99→| Secrets | Secret Manager direct access | External Secrets Operator → K8s Secrets |
   100→| Scaling | 0-10 instances | HPA 1-5 replicas |
   101→| Ingress | Direct HTTPS | Dual: Kong (API) + Gateway API + IAP (frontend) |
   102→| CI/CD | Cloud Build + Bitbucket | GitHub Actions + ArgoCD |
   103→| Health checks | Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK | K8s liveness/readiness probes |
   104→
   105→---
   106→
   107→## 3. Pre-requisites
   108→
   109→Before starting, ensure:
   110→
   111→- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-workloads` GitHub org (write)
   112→- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure` GitOps repo (write)
   113→- [ ] Access to `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows` (read)
   114→- [ ] `kix-platform-modules` base Helm chart published to Artifact Registry
   115→- [ ] ArgoCD ApplicationSet configured with git directory generator
   116→- [ ] Shared Kong gateway operational in `shared-kong` namespace
   117→- [ ] GKE Gateway API + IAP configured on frontend LB
   118→- [ ] External Secrets Operator running in `shared-secrets` namespace
   119→- [ ] GCP Workload Identity pool configured for GitHub Actions OIDC
   120→- [ ] Backstage operational in `shared-backstage` namespace
   121→- [ ] Cloud SQL instance accessible from GKE cluster (private IP, same VPC)
   122→- [ ] GCP service account for IQ Portal workload with IAM roles:
   123→  - `roles/cloudsql.client`
   124→  - `roles/storage.objectAdmin`
   125→  - `roles/aiplatform.user`
   126→  - `roles/cloudtasks.enqueuer`
   127→  - `roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor`
   128→
   129→---
   130→
   131→## Step 1: Create the New Repository
   132→
   133→### 1.1 Create repo from template
   134→
   135→```bash
   136→gh repo create konecta-ix-workloads/kix-usecase-iq-portal \
   137→  --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template \
   138→  --private
   139→```
   140→
   141→### 1.2 Clone and set up
   142→
   143→```bash
   144→git clone git@github.com:konecta-ix-workloads/kix-usecase-iq-portal.git
   145→cd kix-usecase-iq-portal
   146→```
   147→
   148→### 1.3 Copy application code
   149→
   150→Copy the entire application source from the current `kon-iq-portal` repo. Final structure:
   151→
   152→```
   153→kix-usecase-iq-portal/
   154→├── backend/              # Express.js API
   155→├── src/                  # Nuxt 4 frontend
   156→├── public/               # Static assets
   157→├── scripts/              # Deployment scripts
   158→├── nuxt.config.ts
   159→├── package.json
   160→├── package-lock.json
   161→├── tsconfig*.json
   162→├── version.txt
   163→├── Dockerfile            # Modified (see Step 3)
   164→├── docker-entrypoint.sh
   165→├── .dockerignore
   166→├── catalog-info.yaml     # New (Backstage)
   167→├── chart/                # New (Helm chart)
   168→│   ├── Chart.yaml
   169→│   └── values.yaml
   170→├── docs/                 # New (TechDocs)
   171→│   └── index.md
   172→├── .github/workflows/    # New (CI)
   173→│   └── ci.yaml
   174→├── CODEOWNERS
   175→└── .env.example
   176→```
   177→
   178→### 1.4 Remove Cloud Build config
   179→
   180→```bash
   181→rm cloudbuild.yaml
   182→```
   183→
   184→### 1.5 Add CODEOWNERS
   185→
   186→```
   187→# Default owners
   188→* @konecta-ix-workloads/iq-portal-team
   189→```
   190→
   191→### 1.6 Add .dockerignore
   192→
   193→```
   194→node_modules
   195→.nuxt
   196→.output
   197→dist
   198→.git
   199→.github
   200→chart
   201→docs
   202→*.md
   203→.env*
   204→!.env.example
   205→```
   206→
   207→### 1.7 Add catalog-info.yaml
   208→
   209→```yaml
   210→apiVersion: backstage.io/v1alpha1
   211→kind: Component
   212→metadata:
   213→  name: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   214→  description: Konecta IQ Portal — AI-powered knowledge platform
   215→  annotations:
   216→    github.com/project-slug: konecta-ix-workloads/kix-usecase-iq-portal
   217→    argocd/app-name: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   218→    backstage.io/kubernetes-id: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   219→    backstage.io/kubernetes-namespace: dev-api-services,qa-api-services,prod-api-services
   220→    backstage.io/techdocs-ref: dir:.
   221→    sonarqube.org/project-key: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   222→  tags:
   223→    - nodejs
   224→    - express
   225→    - nuxt
   226→    - typescript
   227→    - cloud-sql
   228→spec:
   229→  type: service
   230→  lifecycle: production
   231→  owner: group:iq-portal-team
   232→  system: kix-platform
   233→  dependsOn:
   234→    - resource:cloud-sql-iqplatform
   235→    - component:kix-services-kong
   236→    - component:kix-mcp-gateway

   238→
   239→### 1.8 Add TechDocs
   240→
   241→Create `docs/index.md`:
   242→
   243→```markdown
   244→# IQ Portal
   245→
   246→AI-powered knowledge platform for Konecta.
   247→
   248→## API
   249→
   250→Swagger documentation available at `/docs` on the running service.
   251→
   252→## Runbook
   253→
   254→- Health check: `GET /health` (liveness), `GET /api/health` (readiness)
   255→- Logs: `kubectl logs -n {env}-api-services -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app`
   256→- Database: Cloud SQL `kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform`
   257→```
   258→
   259→Create `mkdocs.yml`:
   260→
   261→```yaml
   262→site_name: IQ Portal
   263→nav:
   264→  - Home: index.md
   265→plugins:
   266→  - techdocs-core
   267→```
   268→
   269→---
   270→
   271→## Step 2: Application Code Changes
   272→
   273→### 2.1 Database connection
   274→
   275→**No code changes required.** The existing `database.ts` supports both Unix socket (Cloud Run) and TCP (K8s):
   276→
   277→```typescript
   278→// backend/db/database.ts — already handles both
   279→const pool = process.env.DATABASE_URL
   280→    ? new Pool({ connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL })
   281→    : new Pool({
   282→        host: process.env.DB_HOST || 'localhost',
   283→        port: parseInt(process.env.DB_PORT || '5432'),
   284→        // ...
   285→    });
   286→```
   287→
   288→On kix, environment variables will be:
   289→```
   290→DB_HOST=localhost        # Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar
   291→DB_PORT=5432
   292→```
   293→
   294→### 2.2 Add root-level health endpoint
   295→
   296→Add to `backend/routes/index.ts`:
   297→
   298→```typescript
   299→// K8S HEALTH PROBES
   300→// Lightweight endpoint for Kubernetes liveness probes
   301→app.get('/health', (req, res) => {
   302→    res.status(200).json({ status: 'ok' });
   303→});
   304→```
   305→
   306→Keep `/api/health` for detailed checks (DB + GCP connectivity).
   307→
   308→### 2.3 Environment variables
   309→
   310→All changes are via Helm values, no code changes:
   311→
   312→| Variable | kix Value |
   313→|----------|-----------|
   314→| `DB_HOST` | `localhost` (sidecar) |
   315→| `DB_PORT` | `5432` |
   316→| `MCP_GATEWAY_URL` | `http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway` |
   317→| `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` | `https://iq-portal.app.konecta.io` |
   318→
   319→---
   320→
   321→## Step 3: Dockerfile Changes
   322→
   323→### 3.1 Pin base image digests
   324→
   325→Replace all `FROM node:22-alpine` with pinned digests:
   326→
   327→```dockerfile
   328→FROM node:22-alpine@sha256:<current-digest> AS dependencies
   329→```
   330→
   331→### 3.2 Remove HEALTHCHECK instruction
   332→
   333→Delete the `HEALTHCHECK` block (lines 142-157) — K8s probes replace it.
   334→
   335→---
   336→
   337→## Step 4: Helm Chart
   338→
   339→### 4.1 Chart.yaml
   340→
   341→```yaml
   342→apiVersion: v2
   343→name: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   344→version: 0.1.0
   345→type: application
   346→
   347→dependencies:
   348→  - name: base-service-chart
   349→    version: "~1.0.0"
   350→    repository: "oci://europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/helm-charts"
   351→```
   352→
   353→### 4.2 values.yaml
   354→
   355→```yaml
   356→base-service-chart:
   357→  nameOverride: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   358→
   359→  image:
   360→    repository: europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-platform/docker/kix-usecase-iq-portal
   361→    tag: "latest"  # Overridden by CI
   362→
   363→  containers:
   364→    app:
   365→      port: 8080
   366→      env:
   367→        NODE_ENV: "production"
   368→        PORT: "8080"
   369→        DB_HOST: "localhost"
   370→        DB_PORT: "5432"
   371→        DB_NAME: "iqplatform"
   372→        GCP_PROJECT_ID: "kd-corp-dgt-svc-apps-dev"
   373→        GCP_LOCATION: "europe-west1"
   374→        VERTEX_AI_PROJECT_ID: "kd-lab-464110"
   375→        VERTEX_AI_LOCATION: "us-central1"
   376→        VERTEX_AI_MODEL: "gemini-2.5-flash"
   377→        RAG_PROJECT_ID: "kd-lab-464110"
   378→        RAG_LOCATION: "europe-west4"
   379→        RAG_CORPUS_ID: "4532873024948404224"
   380→        MCP_GATEWAY_URL: "http://kong.shared-kong.svc.cluster.local/mcp-gateway"
   381→        CLOUD_TASKS_PROJECT_ID: "kd-lab-464110"
   382→        CLOUD_TASKS_QUEUE: "rag-ingestion-poc"
   383→        CLOUD_TASKS_LOCATION: "europe-west1"
   384→        CHANNELHUB_URL: "https://api.konecta.cloud/one-channel-hub/prd"
   385→        CHANNELHUB_LOGIN_URL: "https://ch-prd.auth.eu-west-1.amazoncognito.com"
   386→        LOG_LEVEL: "info"
   387→      envFrom:
   388→        - secretRef:
   389→            name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets
   390→      resources:
   391→        requests:
   392→          cpu: 250m
   393→          memory: 256Mi
   394→        limits:
   395→          cpu: "1"
   396→          memory: 512Mi
   397→
   398→  cloudSqlProxy:
   399→    enabled: true
   400→    instanceConnectionName: "kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform"
   401→    port: 5432
   402→
   403→  serviceAccount:
   404→    create: true
   405→    annotations:
   406→      iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account: kix-iq-portal@kd-ix-platform.iam.gserviceaccount.com
   407→
   408→  probes:
   409→    liveness:
   410→      httpGet:
   411→        path: /health
   412→        port: 8080
   413→      initialDelaySeconds: 30
   414→      periodSeconds: 15
   415→    readiness:
   416→      httpGet:
   417→        path: /api/health
   418→        port: 8080
   419→      initialDelaySeconds: 40
   420→      periodSeconds: 10
   421→    startup:
   422→      httpGet:
   423→        path: /health
   424→        port: 8080
   425→      initialDelaySeconds: 10
   426→      periodSeconds: 5
   427→      failureThreshold: 12
   428→
   429→  autoscaling:
   430→    enabled: true
   431→    minReplicas: 1
   432→    maxReplicas: 5
   433→    targetCPUUtilization: 70
   434→
   435→  services:
   436→    api:
   437→      port: 8080
   438→      ingress:
   439→        class: kong
   440→        path: /api/iq-portal
   441→        stripPath: true
   442→    frontend:
   443→      port: 8080
   444→      ingress:
   445→        class: gateway-api
   446→        host: iq-portal.app.konecta.io
   447→```
   448→

   450→
   451→## Step 5: GitHub Actions CI Workflow
   452→
   453→Create `.github/workflows/ci.yaml`:
   454→
   455→```yaml
   456→name: CI Pipeline
   457→
   458→on:
   459→  push:
   460→    branches: [main, develop, staging]
   461→  pull_request:
   462→    branches: [main]
   463→
   464→permissions:
   465→  contents: read
   466→  id-token: write
   467→  security-events: write
   468→
   469→jobs:
   470→  pipeline:
   471→    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1
   472→    with:
   473→      service-name: kix-usecase-iq-portal
   474→      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile
   475→      chart-path: ./chart
   476→      node-version: "22"
   477→      build-args: |
   478→        VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${{ vars.VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID }}
   479→        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT=${{ vars.ENVIRONMENT }}
   480→        COMMIT_SHA=${{ github.sha }}
   481→      skip-tests: true
   482→    secrets: inherit
   483→```
   484→

   487→## Step 6: Secrets Migration
   488→
   489→### 6.1 Create secrets in GCP Secret Manager
   490→
   491→```bash
   492→PROJECT=kd-ix-platform
   493→
   494→# For each secret:
   495→echo -n "value" | gcloud secrets create kix-iq-portal-db-password \
   496→  --project=$PROJECT --replication-policy=automatic --data-file=-
   497→```
   498→
   499→### 6.2 Create ExternalSecret
   500→
   501→Add to `chart/templates/external-secret.yaml`:
   502→
   503→```yaml
   504→apiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1
   505→kind: ExternalSecret
   506→metadata:
   507→  name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets
   508→spec:
   509→  refreshInterval: 1h
   510→  secretStoreRef:
   511→    name: gcp-secret-store
   512→    kind: SecretStore
   513→  target:
   514→    name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets
   515→    creationPolicy: Owner
   516→  data:
   517→    - secretKey: DB_PASSWORD
   518→      remoteRef:
   519→        key: kix-iq-portal-db-password
   520→    - secretKey: DB_USER
   521→      remoteRef:
   522→        key: kix-iq-portal-db-user
   523→    - secretKey: JWT_SECRET
   524→      remoteRef:
   525→        key: kix-iq-portal-jwt-secret
   526→    - secretKey: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID
   527→      remoteRef:
   528→        key: kix-iq-portal-google-client-id
   529→    - secretKey: RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN
   530→      remoteRef:
   531→        key: kix-iq-portal-rag-pipeline-token
   532→    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID
   533→      remoteRef:
   534→        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-id
   535→    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET
   536→      remoteRef:
   537→        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-secret
   538→    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID
   539→      remoteRef:
   540→        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-connection-id
   541→    - secretKey: GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET
   542→      remoteRef:
   543→        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-image-bucket
   544→    - secretKey: GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET
   545→      remoteRef:
   546→        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-video-bucket
   547→    - secretKey: GCS_RAG_BUCKET
   548→      remoteRef:
   549→        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-rag-bucket
   550→```
