   594→
   595→## Step 8: Database Migration
   596→
   597→### Strategy
   598→
   599→The app runs migrations automatically on startup. This is safe because:
   600→
   601→- The Cloud SQL instance already exists with the schema
   602→- Migrations are idempotent (tracked in `schema_migrations` table)
   603→- No schema changes are part of this migration
   604→
   605→### Connectivity test
   606→
   607→```bash
   608→# Deploy test pod with cloud-sql-proxy
   609→kubectl run sql-test --rm -it \
   610→  --image=gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2.14.3 \
   611→  --namespace=dev-api-services \
   612→  --serviceaccount=kix-usecase-iq-portal \
   613→  -- --private-ip kd-lab-464110:europe-west1:konecta-iqplatform
   614→```
   615→
   616→---
   617→
   618→## Step 9: Verification & Smoke Tests
   619→
   620→### 9.1 Deployment checklist
   621→
   622→```bash
   623→NAMESPACE=dev-api-services
   624→
   625→# 1. Pod running (2/2 = app + cloud-sql-proxy)
   626→kubectl get pods -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal
   627→
   628→# 2. Both containers healthy
   629→kubectl describe pod -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal | grep -A5 "Conditions"
   630→
   631→# 3. Logs check
   632→kubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c app | grep "running on"
   633→
   634→# 4. Cloud SQL proxy connected
   635→kubectl logs -n $NAMESPACE -l app=kix-usecase-iq-portal -c cloud-sql-proxy | grep "ready"
   636→```
   637→
   638→### 9.2 Functional smoke tests
   639→
   640→```bash
   641→BASE_URL="https://iq-portal.dev.konecta.io"
   642→
   643→# Health check
   644→curl -s $BASE_URL/health | jq .
   645→# Expected: {"status":"ok"}
   646→
   647→# Full health check
   648→curl -s $BASE_URL/api/health | jq .
   649→# Expected: {"status":"ok","checks":{"database":"ok","gcp":"ok"}}
   650→
   651→# Version
   652→curl -s $BASE_URL/api/version | jq .
   653→
   654→# Frontend loads
   655→curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" $BASE_URL/
   656→# Expected: 200
   657→
   658→# API auth check
   659→curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" $BASE_URL/api/agents
   660→# Expected: 401
   661→```
   662→
   663→---
   664→
   665→## Step 10: DNS & Domain Cutover
   666→
   667→### New domains
   668→
   669→| Path | Domain |
   670→|------|--------|
   671→| Frontend (browser) | `iq-portal.app.konecta.io` |
   672→| Frontend (dev) | `iq-portal.dev.konecta.io` |
   673→| API (machine) | `api.konecta.io/api/iq-portal` |
   674→
   675→### Decommission Cloud Run
   676→
   677→Once verified:
   678→
   679→```bash
   680→# Stop Cloud Run
   681→gcloud run services update kon-iq-portal \
   682→  --region=europe-west1 \
   683→  --max-instances=0
   684→
   685→# After confidence period (1-2 weeks), delete
   686→gcloud run services delete kon-iq-portal \
   687→  --region=europe-west1
   688→```

   691→
   692→## Appendix A: Full Environment Variable Mapping
   693→
   694→| Variable | Source on Cloud Run | Source on kix |
   695→|----------|-------------------|--------------|
   696→| `NODE_ENV` | Terraform | Helm `env` |
   697→| `PORT` | Default (8080) | Helm `env` |
   698→| `DB_HOST` | Terraform (`/cloudsql/...`) | Helm `env` (`localhost`) |
   699→| `DB_PORT` | Default (5432) | Helm `env` |
   700→| `DB_NAME` | Terraform | Helm `env` |
   701→| `DB_USER` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret |
   702→| `DB_PASSWORD` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret |
   703→| `JWT_SECRET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret |
   704→| `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret |
   705→| `GCP_PROJECT_ID` | Terraform | Helm `env` |
   706→| `GCP_LOCATION` | Terraform | Helm `env` |
   707→| `VERTEX_AI_*` | Terraform | Helm `env` |
   708→| `RAG_*` | Terraform | Helm `env` |
   709→| `RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret |
   710→| `GCS_*_BUCKET` | Secret Manager | ExternalSecret |
   711→| `CLOUD_TASKS_*` | Terraform | Helm `env` |
   712→| `MCP_GATEWAY_URL` | Default | Helm `env` |
   713→| `CHANNELHUB_*` | Terraform/Secret Manager | Helm `env` / ExternalSecret |
   714→| `LOG_LEVEL` | Default | Helm `env` |
   715→| `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` | Terraform | Helm `env` (per-env) |
   716→
   717→---
   718→
   719→## Appendix B: Rollback Procedure
   720→
   721→### Via Git
   722→
   723→```bash
   724→cd kix-platform-infrastructure
   725→git revert HEAD
   726→git push
   727→# ArgoCD syncs automatically
   728→```
   729→
   730→### Via kubectl
   731→
   732→```bash
   733→kubectl rollout undo deployment/kix-usecase-iq-portal -n dev-api-services
   734→```
   735→
   736→### Fall back to Cloud Run
   737→
   738→```bash
   739→gcloud run services update kon-iq-portal \
   740→  --region=europe-west1 \
   741→  --max-instances=10
   742→```
   743→
```

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Update App-Migration Target Architecture

## Context

The app-migration documentation currently describes a model where each service repo contains both application code AND hosting data (Helm chart, CI workflow), with a centralized GitOps repo (`kix-platform-infrastructure`) holding ArgoCD ApplicationSets and per-environment value overrides, and ESO (External Secrets Operator) for secrets management.

The new target architecture separates concerns into two repo types:
- **Code repos** (source only) in their respective orgs (`konecta-ix-apps`, `konecta-ix-mcp-servers`, `konecta-ix-demos`)
- **Workload repos** (hosting data) in `konecta-ix-workloads` — each containing Helm chart, ArgoCD Application definition, and Terraform code

Secrets are managed via Terraform in the workload repo, using GitHub Actions secrets prefixed with `TF_VARS_` for static values. No centralized GitOps repo — ArgoCD watches each workload repo directly.

Branch mapping: `develop` → dev, `main` → qa, `v*` tags → prod.

## Files to Modify

### 1. `docs/app-migration/target-architecture.md` (Primary — major rewrite)

**Repository Model section:**
- Split org descriptions into Code Orgs vs Hosting Org
- Show `konecta-ix-apps`, `konecta-ix-mcp-servers`, `konecta-ix-demos` as code-only repos
- Show `konecta-ix-workloads` as hosting-only repos (one per app/service)
- Remove `kix-platform-infrastructure` as centralized GitOps repo (it stays for platform infra, not app deployments)
- Remove `chart/` from service repo structure
- Remove `kix-platform-ci-workflows` chart-path references

**New "Code Repository Structure" section:**
```
konecta-ix-apps/kix-app-iqportal/
├── src/                      # Application source code
├── Dockerfile                # Multi-stage Docker build
├── catalog-info.yaml         # Backstage service catalog
├── docs/                     # TechDocs content
├── .github/workflows/
│   └── ci.yaml               # Build + push pipeline (develop & main)
└── .env.example
```

**New "Workload Repository Structure" section:**
```
konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-iqportal/
├── helm/                     # Helm chart
│   ├── Chart.yaml
│   ├── values.yaml           # Service defaults
│   └── values-{env}.yaml     # Per-environment overrides
├── argocd/
│   └── application.yaml      # ArgoCD Application manifest
├── terraform/                # Service infrastructure
│   ├── main.tf               # Service account, IAM, secrets
│   ├── variables.tf
│   └── backend.tf
└── .github/workflows/
    └── deploy.yaml           # Terraform apply workflow
```

**CI/CD Flow section:**
- Code repo CI: develop/main push → lint, test, build, scan, sign, push to Artifact Registry
- Workload repo: ArgoCD watches directly, Terraform manages infra
- Update flow diagram to show two-repo model

**Key Design Decisions:**
- Add "Code/hosting separation" rationale
- Update "Centralised GitOps" → "Per-workload GitOps"
- Add "Terraform-managed secrets" rationale

### 2. `docs/app-migration/cicd-pipeline.md` (Moderate updates)

- Update branch triggers to `develop` and `main` (remove `staging`)
- Update Branch → Environment Mapping: develop→dev, main→qa, v* tags→prod
- Remove `chart-path` from example ci.yaml (code repo has no chart/)
- Update Cross-Org Dispatch section — CI pushes image, workload repo ArgoCD detects new tag
- Update "Required Repository Configuration" for the code repo vs workload repo split

### 3. `docs/app-migration/gitops-promotion.md` (Major rewrite)

- Replace centralized GitOps flow with per-workload-repo model
- Update GitOps Repository Structure to show workload repo layout
- Replace ArgoCD ApplicationSet with per-workload ArgoCD Application
- Update promotion workflow — update image tag in workload repo values file
- Update rollback procedures to reference workload repos
- Update ArgoCD Projects section

### 4. `docs/app-migration/kubernetes-helm.md` (Moderate updates)

- Update Three-Layer Architecture → describe chart in workload repo, not code repo
- Layer 1: Base chart (kix-platform-modules) — unchanged
- Layer 2: Service chart now in `konecta-ix-workloads/{workload}/helm/`
- Layer 3: Environment values in same workload repo (`values-{env}.yaml`)
- Update ArgoCD ApplicationSet Strategy → per-workload Application

### 5. `docs/app-migration/security.md` (Moderate updates)

- Replace ESO secrets management with Terraform-based approach
- Add section on GitHub Actions secrets with `TF_VARS_` prefix
- Replace ExternalSecret examples with Terraform `kubernetes_secret` + `google_secret_manager_secret` examples
- Keep Workload Identity section as-is

### 6. `docs/app-migration/backstage-onboarding.md` (Moderate updates)

- Update onboarding flow to create TWO repos (code + workload)
- Update Software Template parameters
- Update manual onboarding steps
- Update onboarding checklist

### 7. `docs/app-migration/appendix-reference.md` (Moderate updates)

- Update Repository & Namespace Mapping table (split code repo from workload repo columns)
- Update Branch → Environment Mapping: develop→dev, main→qa, v* tags→prod
- Update Platform Repositories descriptions

### 8. `docs/app-migration/service-guides/iq-portal.md` (Moderate updates)

- Update repo structure to show two repos (code in konecta-ix-apps, hosting in konecta-ix-workloads)
- Update secrets migration (Step 6) from ESO to Terraform + TF_VARS_
- Update GitOps configuration (Step 7) — workload repo, not centralized
- Update CI workflow to remove chart-path
- Update pre-requisites

### 9. `docs/app-migration/index.md` (Minor updates)

- Update Quick Links to reflect new repo model

## Key Patterns for Secrets

**Auto-generated secrets** (in workload repo Terraform):
```hcl
resource "random_password" "db" {
  length  = 32
  special = true
}

resource "google_secret_manager_secret_version" "db_password" {
  secret=[REDACTED].db_password.id
  secret_data = random_password.db.result
}
```

**Static secrets** (via GitHub Actions → Terraform):
- GitHub repo settings → Secrets → Per-environment secrets prefixed `TF_VARS_`
- Example: `TF_VARS_API_KEY_DEV`, `TF_VARS_API_KEY_QA`, `TF_VARS_API_KEY_PROD`
- Terraform workflow reads these as `TF_VAR_api_key` and creates K8s secrets

```hcl
variable "api_key" {
  type      = string
  sensitive = true
}

resource "kubernetes_secret" "app_secrets" {
  metadata {
    name      = "kix-app-iqportal-secrets"
    namespace = var.namespace
  }
  data = {
    API_KEY     = var.api_key
    DB_PASSWORD = random_password.db.result  # auto-generated
  }
}
```

## Verification

1. Run `npm run build` to validate all links and Docusaurus build
2. Check internal cross-references between updated docs are consistent
3. Verify no broken links to renamed/removed sections

           │\n┌──────────▼───────────────────┐   ┌───────────────▼───────────────────────┐\n│  GCP LB + Cloud Armor (WAF)  │   │  GCP LB + Gateway API + IAP +         │\n│   (API ingress via Kong)     │   │  Cloud Armor (Frontend ingress)       │\n└──────────┬───────────────────┘   └───────────────┬───────────────────────┘\n           │                                       │\n┌──────────▼───────────────────────────────────────▼────────────────────────┐\n│              KIX-INFRASTRUCTURE GKE CLUSTER (eu-west1)                    │\n│                        kd-ix-eur-{env}-gke                                │\n│  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  shared-kong (Kong Gateway)                                        │   │\n│  │    └── routes to {env}-api-services, {env}-mcp-servers             │   │\n│  │    └── routes to shared-litellm (LLM Proxy) → internal only        │   │\n│  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                           │\n│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │\n│  │  Application Namespaces (per environment × domain)                  │  │\n│  │                                                                     │  │\n│  │  {env}-api-services     │ FastAPI backends, business apps,          │  │\n│  │                           bots, data pipelines                      │  │\n│  │  {env}-mcp-servers      │ MCP protocol servers (SSE)                │  │\n│  │                                                                     │  │\n│  │  {env}-web-apps         │ Nuxt 3 frontends  → Frontend LB           │  │\n│  │                           (GKE Gateway API + IAM auth)              │  │\n│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │\n│  ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  shared-monitoring: Prometheus / Grafana / Jaeger / OTel           │   │\n│  └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                           │\n│  ┌───────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐ │\n│  │ shared-litellm│  │ shared-argocd│  │ shared-secrets│  │  shared-     │ │\n│  │ (behind Kong) │  │ (GitOps CD)  │  │ (ESO + CSI)   │  │  backstage   │ │\n│  └───────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └───────────────┘  └──────────────┘ │\n└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n         │                    │                    │                    │\n         ▼                    ▼                    ▼                    ▼\n┌─────────────┐  ┌───────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────┐ ┌─────────────────┐\n│  Cloud SQL  │  │  Vertex AI / GCS  │  │  GCP Secret Manager │ │  Cloud Storage  │\n│ (PostgreSQL)│  │  Pub/Sub / APIs   │  │                     │ │    (Buckets)    │\n└─────────────┘  └───────────────────┘  └─────────────────────┘ └─────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Dual Ingress Architecture\n\nTwo separate ingress paths with independent load balancers:\n\n| Path | Flow | Purpose |\n|------|------|---------|\n| **API** | Internet → GCP LB + Cloud Armor → Kong Ingress → `{env}-api-services` / `{env}-mcp-servers` | Machine-to-machine, API consumers |\n| **Frontend** | Internet → GCP LB + Cloud Armor + GKE Gateway API + IAP → `{env}-web-apps` | Browser users, human authentication |\n\n## Repository Model\n\nServices follow a **two-repo pattern**: application source code and deployment hosting data are kept in separate repositories across different GitHub organisations.\n\n### Organisation Structure\n\n```\nGitHub Enterprise: Konecta Digital\n│\n├── CODE REPOSITORIES (source code + CI only)\n│   │\n│   ├── Org: konecta-ix-apps (Business Applications)\n│   │   ├── kix-app-iqportal              # IQ Portal source code\n│   │   ├── kix-app-hrfraud               # HR Fraud source code\n│   │   ├── kix-app-hrportal              # HR Portal source code\n│   │   └── kix-app-*                     # One repo per application\n│   │\n│   ├── Org: konecta-ix-mcp-servers (MCP Integration Team)\n│   │   ├── kix-mcp-gateway               # MCP Gateway source code\n│   │   ├── kix-mcp-ragengine             # RAG Engine source code\n│   │   └── kix-mcp-*                     # One repo per MCP server (~14 repos)\n│   │\n│   └── Org: konecta-ix-demos (Demo Team)\n│       └── kix-demo-*                    # Demo/prototype source code\n│\n├── WORKLOAD REPOSITORIES (hosting data — Helm, ArgoCD, Terraform)\n│   │\n│   └── Org: konecta-ix-workloads (Deployment & Operations)\n│       ├── kix-workload-iqportal         # IQ Portal hosting data\n│       ├── kix-workload-hrfraud          # HR Fraud hosting data\n│       ├── kix-workload-mcp-gateway      # MCP Gateway hosting data\n│       ├── kix-workload-mcp-ragengine    # RAG Engine hosting data\n│       └── kix-workload-*                # One per deployed service\n│\n├── PLATFORM REPOSITORIES (shared tooling)\n│   │\n│   └── Org: konecta-ix-platform (Platform Engineering)\n│       ├── kix-platform-infrastructure   # GCP infra, GKE clusters, platform ArgoCD\n│       ├── kix-platform-modules          # Shared Terraform modules + Helm base chart\n│       ├── kix-platform-ci-workflows     # Shared reusable GitHub Actions workflows\n│       ├── kix-platform-service-template # Template repos for bootstrapping\n│       ├── kix-platform-backstage        # Backstage config, plugins, catalog\n│       └── kix-platform-docs             # Documentation (TechDocs)\n│\n└── Org: konecta-ix-services (Platform Services)\n    ├── kix-services-kong\n    ├── kix-services-litellm\n    ├── kix-services-monitoring\n    └── kix-services-backstage\n```\n\n### Code Repository Structure\n\nEach code repository contains **only application source code** and a CI pipeline that builds and publishes a Docker container:\n\n```\nkonecta-ix-apps/kix-app-iqportal/\n├── src/                          # Application source code\n├── Dockerfile                    # Multi-stage Docker build\n├── catalog-info.yaml             # Backstage service catalog descriptor\n├── docs/                         # TechDocs content (Backstage)\n│   └── index.md\n├── .github/workflows/\n│   └── ci.yaml                   # Build + push pipeline (develop & main)\n├── CODEOWNERS\n├── .dockerignore\n└── .env.example\n```\n\n**CI pipeline** triggers automatically on `develop` and `main` branches:\n- Calls reusable workflows from `kix-platform-ci-workflows`\n- Builds a Docker container and pushes to GCP Artifact Registry\n- Tags images with `sha-{7-char}` (immutable) and `v{semver}` (on git tags)\n\n### Workload Repository Structure\n\nEach workload repository contains **all deployment and infrastructure data** for a single service:\n\n```\nkonecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-iqportal/\n├── helm/                         # Helm chart\n│   ├── Chart.yaml                # Depends on base-service-chart (OCI)\n│   ├── values.yaml               # Service defaults\n│   ├── values-dev.yaml           # Dev environment overrides\n│   ├── values-qa.yaml            # QA environment overrides\n│   ├── values-prod.yaml          # Prod environment overrides\n│   └── templates/                # Only if custom K8s resources needed\n├── argocd/\n│   └── application.yaml          # ArgoCD Application manifest\n├── terraform/                    # Service infrastructure (per-env)\n│   ├── main.tf                   # Service account, IAM, secrets, DB\n│   ├── variables.tf              # Input variables (incl. TF_VARS_ secrets)\n│   ├── outputs.tf\n│   └── backend.tf                # Remote state in GCS\n└── .github/workflows/\n    └── deploy.yaml               # Terraform validate-plan-apply workflow\n```\n\n**ArgoCD** watches each workload repo directly and syncs Helm releases from the `helm/` directory.\n\n**Terraform** manages per-service GCP resources (service accounts, IAM, secrets, databases) and is applied via GitHub Actions.\n\n### Secrets Management in Workload Repos\n\nSecrets follow a **Terraform-first approach** with two patterns:\n\n#### Auto-Generated Secrets\n\nSecrets that can be generated (database passwords, API keys) are created by Terraform:\n\n```hcl\nresource \"random_password\" \"db\" {\n  length  = 32\n  special = true\n}\n\nresource \"google_secret_manager_secret_version\" \"db_password\" {\n  secret=[REDACTED].db_password.id\n  secret_data = random_password.db.result\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"app_secrets\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"kix-app-iqportal-secrets\"\n    namespace = var.namespace\n  }\n  data = {\n    DB_PASSWORD = random_password.db.result\n  }\n}\n```\n\n#### Static Secrets (via GitHub Actions)\n\nSecrets that must be provided externally (third-party API keys, OAuth credentials) are stored as **GitHub Actions secrets** on the workload repo, prefixed with `TF_VARS_`:\n\n| GitHub Secret | Terraform Variable | Description |\n|---------------|-------------------|-------------|\n| `TF_VARS_API_KEY` | `var.api_key` | Third-party API key |\n| `TF_VARS_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` | `var.oauth_client_secret` | OAuth credential |\n| `TF_VARS_CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID` | `var.channelhub_client_id` | External service credential |\n\nPer-environment secrets are defined using **GitHub Environments** (dev, qa, prod) on the workload repo. The Terraform workflow passes these as `-var` flags:\n\n```yaml\n# .github/workflows/deploy.yaml (simplified)\njobs:\n  terraform:\n    environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}\n    steps:\n      - uses: hashicorp/setup-terraform@v3\n      - run: |\n          terraform plan \\\n            -var=\"api_key=${{ secrets.TF_VARS_API_KEY }}\" \\\n            -var=\"namespace=${{ vars.NAMESPACE }}\"\n```\n\nTerraform then creates Kubernetes secrets in GCP Secret Manager and/or directly:\n\n```hcl\nvariable \"api_key\" {\n  type      = string\n  sensitive = true\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"app_secrets\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"kix-app-iqportal-secrets\"\n    namespace = var.namespace\n  }\n  data = {\n    API_KEY     = var.api_key            # Static — from TF_VARS_\n    DB_PASSWORD = random_password.db.result  # Auto-generated\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## CI/CD Flow\n\n```\nCODE REPO (konecta-ix-apps or konecta-ix-mcp-servers)\n─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nDeveloper Push → Code Repo (any code org)\n  (e.g. konecta-ix-apps/kix-app-iqportal)\n       │\n       ▼  CI triggers on develop / main branches\n       Calls reusable workflows from:\n       konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows@v1\n       ├── Lint + Unit Tests\n       ├── Secret Scan (Trivy)\n       ├── SCA (Dependency-Track)\n       ├── SAST (SonarQube)\n       ├── Docker Build (BuildKit, multi-stage)\n       ├── Container Scan + SBOM (Trivy + Syft)\n       ├── Push to Artifact Registry\n       └── Cosign Sign (OIDC keyless)\n\nWORKLOAD REPO (konecta-ix-workloads)\n─────────────────────────────────────\n  kix-workload-iqportal\n       │\n       ├── Terraform (via GitHub Actions deploy.yaml)\n       │   ├── Creates GCP service accounts + IAM\n       │   ├── Creates secrets (auto-generated + TF_VARS_)\n       │   └── Creates K8s secrets from Secret Manager\n       │\n       ├── ArgoCD watches this repo directly\n       │   ├── Detects changes to helm/ or argocd/\n       │   └── Syncs Helm release to target namespace\n       │\n       └── Image tag update (manual or automated)\n           ├── Update values-{env}.yaml with new image tag\n           ├── Dev: auto-sync on commit\n           ├── QA: manual sync (08:00-18:00 UTC)\n           └── Prod: manual sync (Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)\n```\n\n### Branch → Environment Mapping\n\n| Branch / Trigger | Environment | Auto-Sync |\n|------------------|-------------|-----------|\n| `develop` (code repo) | dev | Yes |\n| `main` (code repo) | qa | Yes |\n| `v*` tags (code repo) | prod | Manual |\n\n## Key Design Decisions\n\n| Decision | Rationale |\n|----------|-----------|\n| **Code/hosting separation** | Clear ownership boundaries, independent release cycles, security isolation between source code and deployment config |\n| **Per-workload GitOps** | Each team owns their deployment, no bottleneck on a centralized repo, self-service infrastructure |\n| **Repo-per-service** | Team autonomy, clear ownership, independent release cycles |\n| **Terraform-managed secrets** | Secrets are version-controlled (encrypted), auditable, auto-generated where possible, no manual Secret Manager operations |\n| **TF_VARS\\_ prefix convention** | Clear separation of Terraform variables from other GitHub secrets, per-environment via GitHub Environments |\n| **Shared base Helm chart** | Consistency, reduced maintenance, enforced standards |\n| **Cross-org workflow sharing** | DRY principle, single place to update pipeline logic |\n| **Dual ingress** | Different auth models for APIs vs. frontends |\n| **No service mesh (initially)** | Reduce complexity during migration; revisit post-migration |\n\n## Key Differences from Cloud Run\n\n| Aspect | Cloud Run | kix-infrastructure |\n|--------|-----------|-------------------|\n| **DB connection** | Unix socket via VPC connector | Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (TCP) |\n| **Secrets** | Secret Manager direct access | Terraform-managed K8s Secrets (auto-generated + TF_VARS_) |\n| **Scaling** | 0-10 instances (cold starts) | HPA 1-5+ replicas (always warm) |\n| **Ingress** | Direct HTTPS | Dual: Kong (API) + Gateway API + IAP (frontend) |\n| **CI/CD** | Cloud Build + Bitbucket | GitHub Actions + ArgoCD |\n| **Health checks** | Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK | K8s liveness/readiness probes |\n| **Observability** | Fragmented | Unified Prometheus/Grafana/Jaeger |\n| **Deployment config** | Terraform in same repo | Separate workload repo (Helm + ArgoCD + Terraform) |\n\n---\n\n**Next**: [Migration Phases](migration-phases) — Phased approach to migration.\n"
}
```

deployment hosting data are kept in separate repositories across different GitHub organisations.\n\n### Organisation Structure\n\n```\nGitHub Enterprise: Konecta Digital\n│\n├── CODE REPOSITORIES (source code + CI only)\n│   │\n│   ├── Org: konecta-ix-apps (Business Applications)\n│   │   ├── kix-app-iqportal              # IQ Portal source code\n│   │   ├── kix-app-hrfraud               # HR Fraud source code\n│   │   ├── kix-app-hrportal              # HR Portal source code\n│   │   └── kix-app-*                     # One repo per application\n│   │\n│   ├── Org: konecta-ix-mcp-servers (MCP Integration Team)\n│   │   ├── kix-mcp-gateway               # MCP Gateway source code\n│   │   ├── kix-mcp-ragengine             # RAG Engine source code\n│   │   └── kix-mcp-*                     # One repo per MCP server (~14 repos)\n│   │\n│   └── Org: konecta-ix-demos (Demo Team)\n│       └── kix-demo-*                    # Demo/prototype source code\n│\n├── WORKLOAD REPOSITORIES (hosting data — Helm, ArgoCD, Terraform)\n│   │\n│   └── Org: konecta-ix-workloads (Deployment & Operations)\n│       ├── kix-workload-iqportal         # IQ Portal hosting data\n│       ├── kix-workload-hrfraud          # HR Fraud hosting data\n│       ├── kix-workload-mcp-gateway      # MCP Gateway hosting data\n│       ├── kix-workload-mcp-ragengine    # RAG Engine hosting data\n│       └── kix-workload-*                # One per deployed service\n│\n├── PLATFORM REPOSITORIES (shared tooling)\n│   │\n│   └── Org: konecta-ix-platform (Platform Engineering)\n│       ├── kix-platform-infrastructure   # GCP infra, GKE clusters, platform ArgoCD\n│       ├── kix-platform-modules          # Shared Terraform modules + Helm base chart\n│       ├── kix-platform-ci-workflows     # Shared reusable GitHub Actions workflows\n│       ├── kix-platform-service-template # Template repos for bootstrapping\n│       ├── kix-platform-backstage        # Backstage config, plugins, catalog\n│       └── kix-platform-docs             # Documentation (TechDocs)\n│\n└── Org: konecta-ix-services (Platform Services)\n    ├── kix-services-kong\n    ├── kix-services-litellm\n    ├── kix-services-monitoring\n    └── kix-services-backstage\n```\n\n### Code Repository Structure\n\nEach code repository contains **only application source code** and a CI pipeline that builds and publishes a Docker container:\n\n```\nkonecta-ix-apps/kix-app-iqportal/\n├── src/                          # Application source code\n├── Dockerfile                    # Multi-stage Docker build\n├── catalog-info.yaml             # Backstage service catalog descriptor\n├── docs/                         # TechDocs content (Backstage)\n│   └── index.md\n├── .github/workflows/\n│   └── ci.yaml                   # Build + push pipeline (develop & main)\n├── CODEOWNERS\n├── .dockerignore\n└── .env.example\n```\n\n**CI pipeline** triggers automatically on `develop` and `main` branches:\n- Calls reusable workflows from `kix-platform-ci-workflows`\n- Builds a Docker container and pushes to GCP Artifact Registry\n- Tags images with `sha-{7-char}` (immutable) and `v{semver}` (on git tags)\n\n### Workload Repository Structure\n\nEach workload repository contains **all deployment and infrastructure data** for a single service:\n\n```\nkonecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-iqportal/\n├── helm/                         # Helm chart\n│   ├── Chart.yaml                # Depends on base-service-chart (OCI)\n│   ├── values.yaml               # Service defaults\n│   ├── values-dev.yaml           # Dev environment overrides\n│   ├── values-qa.yaml            # QA environment overrides\n│   ├── values-prod.yaml          # Prod environment overrides\n│   └── templates/                # Only if custom K8s resources needed\n├── argocd/\n│   └── application.yaml          # ArgoCD Application manifest\n├── terraform/                    # Service infrastructure (per-env)\n│   ├── main.tf                   # Service account, IAM, secrets, DB\n│   ├── variables.tf              # Input variables (incl. TF_VARS_ secrets)\n│   ├── outputs.tf\n│   └── backend.tf                # Remote state in GCS\n└── .github/workflows/\n    └── deploy.yaml               # Terraform validate-plan-apply workflow\n```\n\n**ArgoCD** watches each workload repo directly and syncs Helm releases from the `helm/` directory.\n\n**Terraform** manages per-service GCP resources (service accounts, IAM, secrets, databases) and is applied via GitHub Actions.\n\n### Secrets Management in Workload Repos\n\nSecrets follow a **Terraform-first approach** with two patterns:\n\n#### Auto-Generated Secrets\n\nSecrets that can be generated (database passwords, API keys) are created by Terraform:\n\n```hcl\nresource \"random_password\" \"db\" {\n  length  = 32\n  special = true\n}\n\nresource \"google_secret_manager_secret_version\" \"db_password\" {\n  secret=[REDACTED].db_password.id\n  secret_data = random_password.db.result\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"app_secrets\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"kix-app-iqportal-secrets\"\n    namespace = var.namespace\n  }\n  data = {\n    DB_PASSWORD = random_password.db.result\n  }\n}\n```\n\n#### Static Secrets (via GitHub Actions)\n\nSecrets that must be provided externally (third-party API keys, OAuth credentials) are stored as **GitHub Actions secrets** on the workload repo, prefixed with `TF_VARS_`:\n\n| GitHub Secret | Terraform Variable | Description |\n|---------------|-------------------|-------------|\n| `TF_VARS_API_KEY` | `var.api_key` | Third-party API key |\n| `TF_VARS_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` | `var.oauth_client_secret` | OAuth credential |\n| `TF_VARS_CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID` | `var.channelhub_client_id` | External service credential |\n\nPer-environment secrets are defined using **GitHub Environments** (dev, qa, prod) on the workload repo. The Terraform workflow passes these as `-var` flags:\n\n```yaml\n# .github/workflows/deploy.yaml (simplified)\njobs:\n  terraform:\n    environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}\n    steps:\n      - uses: hashicorp/setup-terraform@v3\n      - run: |\n          terraform plan \\\n            -var=\"api_key=${{ secrets.TF_VARS_API_KEY }}\" \\\n            -var=\"namespace=${{ vars.NAMESPACE }}\"\n```\n\nTerraform then creates Kubernetes secrets from both auto-generated and static values:\n\n```hcl\nvariable \"api_key\" {\n  type      = string\n  sensitive = true\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"app_secrets\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"kix-app-iqportal-secrets\"\n    namespace = var.namespace\n  }\n  data = {\n    API_KEY     = var.api_key                # Static — from TF_VARS_\n    DB_PASSWORD = random_password.db.result   # Auto-generated\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## CI/CD Flow\n\n```\nCODE REPO (konecta-ix-apps or konecta-ix-mcp-servers)\n─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nDeveloper Push → Code Repo (any code org)\n  (e.g. konecta-ix-apps/kix-app-iqportal)\n       │\n       ▼  CI triggers on develop / main branches\n       Calls reusable workflows from:\n       konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows@v1\n       ├── Lint + Unit Tests\n       ├── Secret Scan (Trivy)\n       ├── SCA (Dependency-Track)\n       ├── SAST (SonarQube)\n       ├── Docker Build (BuildKit, multi-stage)\n       ├── Container Scan + SBOM (Trivy + Syft)\n       ├── Push to Artifact Registry\n       └── Cosign Sign (OIDC keyless)\n\nWORKLOAD REPO (konecta-ix-workloads)\n─────────────────────────────────────\n  kix-workload-iqportal\n       │\n       ├── Terraform (via GitHub Actions deploy.yaml)\n       │   ├── Creates GCP service accounts + IAM\n       │   ├── Creates secrets (auto-generated + TF_VARS_)\n       │   └── Creates K8s secrets in target namespace\n       │\n       ├── ArgoCD watches this repo directly\n       │   ├── Detects changes to helm/ or argocd/\n       │   └── Syncs Helm release to target namespace\n       │\n       └── Image tag update (manual or automated)\n           ├── Update values-{env}.yaml with new image tag\n           ├── Dev: auto-sync on commit\n           ├── QA: manual sync (08:00-18:00 UTC)\n           └── Prod: manual sync (Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)\n```\n\n### Branch → Environment Mapping\n\n| Branch / Trigger | Environment | Auto-Sync |\n|------------------|-------------|-----------|\n| `develop` (code repo) | dev | Yes |\n| `main` (code repo) | qa | Yes |\n| `v*` tags (code repo) | prod | Manual |\n\n## Key Design Decisions\n\n| Decision | Rationale |\n|----------|-----------|\n| **Code/hosting separation** | Clear ownership boundaries, independent release cycles, security isolation between source code and deployment config |\n| **Per-workload GitOps** | Each team owns their deployment, no bottleneck on a centralized repo, self-service infrastructure |\n| **Repo-per-service** | Team autonomy, clear ownership, independent release cycles |\n| **Terraform-managed secrets** | Secrets are version-controlled (encrypted), auditable, auto-generated where possible, no manual Secret Manager operations |\n| **TF_VARS\\_ prefix convention** | Clear separation of Terraform variables from other GitHub secrets, per-environment via GitHub Environments |\n| **Shared base Helm chart** | Consistency, reduced maintenance, enforced standards |\n| **Cross-org workflow sharing** | DRY principle, single place to update pipeline logic |\n| **Dual ingress** | Different auth models for APIs vs. frontends |\n| **No service mesh (initially)** | Reduce complexity during migration; revisit post-migration |\n\n## Key Differences from Cloud Run\n\n| Aspect | Cloud Run | kix-infrastructure |\n|--------|-----------|-------------------|\n| **DB connection** | Unix socket via VPC connector | Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (TCP) |\n| **Secrets** | Secret Manager direct access | Terraform-managed K8s Secrets (auto-generated + TF_VARS_) |\n| **Scaling** | 0-10 instances (cold starts) | HPA 1-5+ replicas (always warm) |\n| **Ingress** | Direct HTTPS | Dual: Kong (API) + Gateway API + IAP (frontend) |\n| **CI/CD** | Cloud Build + Bitbucket | GitHub Actions + ArgoCD |\n| **Health checks** | Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK | K8s liveness/readiness probes |\n| **Observability** | Fragmented | Unified Prometheus/Grafana/Jaeger |\n| **Deployment config** | Terraform in same repo | Separate workload repo (Helm + ArgoCD + Terraform) |\n\n---\n\n**Next**: [Migration Phases](migration-phases) — Phased approach to migration.\n"
}

   255→       ▼  CI triggers on develop / main branches
   256→       Calls reusable workflows from:
   257→       konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows@v1
   258→       ├── Lint + Unit Tests
   259→       ├── Secret Scan (Trivy)
   260→       ├── SCA (Dependency-Track)
   261→       ├── SAST (SonarQube)
   262→       ├── Docker Build (BuildKit, multi-stage)
   263→       ├── Container Scan + SBOM (Trivy + Syft)
   264→       ├── Push to Artifact Registry
   265→       └── Cosign Sign (OIDC keyless)
   266→
   267→WORKLOAD REPO (konecta-ix-workloads)
   268→─────────────────────────────────────
   269→  kix-workload-iqportal
   270→       │
   271→       ├── Terraform (via GitHub Actions deploy.yaml)
   272→       │   ├── Creates GCP service accounts + IAM
   273→       │   ├── Creates secrets (auto-generated + TF_VARS_)
   274→       │   └── Creates K8s secrets in target namespace
   275→       │
   276→       ├── ArgoCD watches this repo directly
   277→       │   ├── Detects changes to helm/ or argocd/
   278→       │   └── Syncs Helm release to target namespace
   279→       │
   280→       └── Image tag update (manual or automated)
   281→           ├── Update values-{env}.yaml with new image tag
   282→           ├── Dev: auto-sync on commit
   283→           ├── QA: manual sync (08:00-18:00 UTC)
   284→           └── Prod: manual sync (Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)
   285→```
   286→
   287→### Branch → Environment Mapping
   288→
   289→| Branch / Trigger | Environment | Auto-Sync |
   290→|------------------|-------------|-----------|
   291→| `develop` (code repo) | dev | Yes |
   292→| `main` (code repo) | qa | Yes |
   293→| `v*` tags (code repo) | prod | Manual |
   294→
   295→## Key Design Decisions
   296→
   297→| Decision | Rationale |
   298→|----------|-----------|
   299→| **Code/hosting separation** | Clear ownership boundaries, independent release cycles, security isolation between source code and deployment config |
   300→| **Per-workload GitOps** | Each team owns their deployment, no bottleneck on a centralized repo, self-service infrastructure |
   301→| **Repo-per-service** | Team autonomy, clear ownership, independent release cycles |
   302→| **Terraform-managed secrets** | Secrets are version-controlled (encrypted), auditable, auto-generated where possible, no manual Secret Manager operations |
   303→| **TF_VARS\_ prefix convention** | Clear separation of Terraform variables from other GitHub secrets, per-environment via GitHub Environments |
   304→| **Shared base Helm chart** | Consistency, reduced maintenance, enforced standards |
   305→| **Cross-org workflow sharing** | DRY principle, single place to update pipeline logic |
   306→| **Dual ingress** | Different auth models for APIs vs. frontends |
   307→| **No service mesh (initially)** | Reduce complexity during migration; revisit post-migration |
   308→
   309→## Key Differences from Cloud Run
   310→
   311→| Aspect | Cloud Run | kix-infrastructure |
   312→|--------|-----------|-------------------|
   313→| **DB connection** | Unix socket via VPC connector | Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar (TCP) |
   314→| **Secrets** | Secret Manager direct access | Terraform-managed K8s Secrets (auto-generated + TF_VARS_) |
   315→| **Scaling** | 0-10 instances (cold starts) | HPA 1-5+ replicas (always warm) |
   316→| **Ingress** | Direct HTTPS | Dual: Kong (API) + Gateway API + IAP (frontend) |
   317→| **CI/CD** | Cloud Build + Bitbucket | GitHub Actions + ArgoCD |
   318→| **Health checks** | Dockerfile HEALTHCHECK | K8s liveness/readiness probes |
   319→| **Observability** | Fragmented | Unified Prometheus/Grafana/Jaeger |
   320→| **Deployment config** | Terraform in same repo | Separate workload repo (Helm + ArgoCD + Terraform) |
   321→
   322→---
   323→
   324→**Next**: [Migration Phases](migration-phases) — Phased approach to migration.
   325→
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs/docs/app-migration/cicd-pipeline.md",
  "content": "---\ntitle: CI/CD Pipeline\nsidebar_label: CI/CD Pipeline\nsidebar_position: 8\ndescription: GitHub Actions pipeline stages, security gates, and scanning\n---\n\n# CI/CD Pipeline\n\nThis document covers the GitHub Actions CI/CD pipeline architecture, stages, and security gates.\n\n## Pipeline Architecture\n\nThe platform uses a **two-repo CI/CD model**:\n\n- **Code repos** (konecta-ix-apps, konecta-ix-mcp-servers, konecta-ix-demos) run the **build pipeline** — lint, test, build, scan, sign, and push Docker images to Artifact Registry\n- **Workload repos** (konecta-ix-workloads) run the **deploy pipeline** — Terraform apply for infrastructure and secrets, with ArgoCD handling Helm-based deployments\n\n### Shared Workflows Repository\n\n```\nkix-platform-ci-workflows/\n└── .github/workflows/\n    ├── standard-pipeline.yaml      # Main orchestrator (code repos)\n    ├── _lint-and-test.yaml         # Language-aware lint + unit tests\n    ├── _secret-scan.yaml           # Trivy filesystem secret detection\n    ├── _sca.yaml                   # Dependency-Track SCA analysis\n    ├── _sast.yaml                  # SonarQube static analysis\n    ├── _build-image.yaml           # Docker build + BuildKit cache\n    ├── _container-scan-sign.yaml   # Trivy image scan + SBOM + Cosign\n    ├── _push-registry.yaml         # Push to Artifact Registry\n    ├── _promote.yaml               # Promote between environments\n    └── _dast.yaml                  # OWASP ZAP baseline scan\n```\n\n### Code Repo CI Workflow\n\nEvery code repo uses this minimal workflow file to call the shared pipeline:\n\n```yaml\n# .github/workflows/ci.yaml\nname: CI\non:\n  push:\n    branches: [develop, main]\n  pull_request:\n    branches: [main]\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n  id-token: write\n  security-events: write\n\njobs:\n  pipeline:\n    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1\n    with:\n      service-name: ${{ github.event.repository.name }}\n      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile\n      python-version: \"3.11\"     # or node-version: \"22\"\n    secrets: inherit\n```\n\n:::note\nCode repos do **not** contain Helm charts or deployment configuration. The `chart-path` parameter is not used. All deployment data lives in the corresponding workload repo.\n:::\n\n### Workload Repo Deploy Workflow\n\nEach workload repo has a Terraform deploy workflow:\n\n```yaml\n# .github/workflows/deploy.yaml\nname: Deploy\non:\n  workflow_dispatch:\n    inputs:\n      environment:\n        description: Target environment\n        required: true\n        type: choice\n        options: [dev, qa, prod]\n\njobs:\n  terraform:\n    environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}\n    steps:\n      - uses: hashicorp/setup-terraform@v3\n      - uses: google-github-actions/auth@v2\n        with:\n          workload_identity_provider: ${{ secrets.WIF_PROVIDER }}\n          service_account: ${{ secrets.WIF_SA }}\n      - run: |\n          cd terraform\n          terraform init\n          terraform plan \\\n            -var=\"environment=${{ inputs.environment }}\" \\\n            -var=\"api_key=${{ secrets.TF_VARS_API_KEY }}\"\n          terraform apply -auto-approve\n```\n\n## Pipeline Stages\n\n### Code Pipeline (Every PR and Merge)\n\n```\n┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐\n│  1. LINT &   │──│  2. SECRET   │──│  3. SCA      │──│  4. SAST     │──│  5. BUILD &  │\n│     TEST     │  │     SCAN     │  │              │  │              │  │  UNIT TEST   │\n└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘\n  PR + merge        PR + merge        PR + merge        PR + merge        merge only\n  • ruff/eslint     • Trivy fs        • Syft SBOM       • SonarQube       • BuildKit\n  • type checks     • --scanners      • generation      • analysis        • multi-stage\n  • pip audit/        secret          • Dependency-     • quality gate:   • unit tests\n    npm audit       • stops on          Track upload     no critical/    • OCI labels\n                      any finding     • stops on           high issues\n                                        critical/high\n\n┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐\n│  6. CONTAINER│──│  7. PUSH &   │\n│  SCAN & SIGN │  │  REGISTRY    │\n└──────────────┘  └──────────────┘\n  merge only        merge only\n  • Trivy image     • Push to AR\n  • scan            • Tag: sha-xxx\n  • SBOM attach     • Tag: v1.2.3\n  • (Syft)            (on git tag)\n  • Cosign sign\n    (OIDC)\n```\n\n### When Stages Run\n\n| Stage | On PR | On Merge | Purpose |\n|-------|-------|----------|---------|\n| 1. Lint & Test | ✓ | ✓ | Fast feedback on code quality |\n| 2. Secret Scan | ✓ | ✓ | Block secrets before commit |\n| 3. SCA | ✓ | ✓ | Dependency vulnerability check |\n| 4. SAST | ✓ | ✓ | Static code analysis |\n| 5. Build & Test | ✗ | ✓ | Docker image build |\n| 6. Container Scan | ✗ | ✓ | Image vulnerability scan + sign |\n| 7. Push | ✗ | ✓ | Push to Artifact Registry |\n\n:::note\nStage 8 (GitOps update) from the previous centralized model has been replaced. Image tag updates are now made directly in the workload repo's `values-{env}.yaml` file, either manually or via automation.\n:::\n\n## Security Gates\n\nAll gates are **fail-stop** — pipeline halts immediately on critical/high findings.\n\n### Gate Details\n\n| Gate | Tool | Stage | Trigger | Action on Failure |\n|------|------|-------|---------|-------------------|\n| **Secret Detection** | Trivy (`--scanners secret`) | 2 | Every PR + merge | Stop pipeline, no exceptions |\n| **Dependency Vulnerabilities** | Dependency-Track (via Syft SBOM) | 3 | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |\n| **SAST Quality Gate** | SonarQube | 4 | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |\n| **Container Vulnerabilities** | Trivy (image scan) | 6 | Every merge | Stop on critical/high |\n| **Image Signature** | Cosign (Sigstore/OIDC) | 6 | Every merge | Unsigned images blocked |\n| **DAST Baseline** | OWASP ZAP | Post-deploy | Before prod promotion | Block promotion on findings |\n| **Admission Control** | Kyverno | Deploy-time | Every sync | Reject unsigned images |\n\n### Tool Configuration\n\n#### Trivy Secret Scan\n\nScans source filesystem before any build:\n\n```yaml\n- name: Secret Scan\n  run: |\n    trivy fs --scanners secret --exit-code 1 .\n```\n\nZero tolerance — any finding stops the pipeline.\n\n#### SonarQube\n\nHosted in `shared-devsecops` namespace:\n\n- Runs static analysis on every PR\n- Quality gate: no new critical or high severity issues\n- Results posted as PR comment\n\n#### Dependency-Track\n\nSBOM-based vulnerability tracking:\n\n- Syft generates CycloneDX SBOMs\n- Uploaded to Dependency-Track for continuous monitoring\n- Pipeline stops on critical/high vulnerabilities\n\n#### Cosign Image Signing\n\nKeyless signing via GitHub OIDC → Sigstore:\n\n```yaml\n- name: Sign Image\n  run: |\n    cosign sign --yes $IMAGE_REF\n```\n\nNo long-lived keys — fully automatic.\n\n### Vulnerability Remediation SLAs\n\nPer the Konecta DevSecOps Handbook:\n\n| Severity | Detection → Triage | Triage → Fix | Fix → Deploy |\n|----------|-------------------|--------------|--------------|\n| Critical | 4 hours | 24 hours | 48 hours |\n| High | 24 hours | 7 days | 14 days |\n| Medium | 7 days | 30 days | 45 days |\n| Low | 30 days | 90 days | Next release |\n\n## Image Tagging Strategy\n\n| Tag | When | Purpose |\n|-----|------|---------|\n| `sha-{7-char}` | Every build | Immutable build identifier |\n| `v{major}.{minor}.{patch}` | On git tag | Release version |\n| `{env}-latest` | On promotion | Environment pointer (mutable) |\n\n**Rule**: SHA tags are immutable. Never overwrite. Promotion copies the SHA tag reference, not the image.\n\n## Secrets Management (CI)\n\n### GitHub OIDC → GCP Workload Identity\n\nNo JSON keys — fully automated authentication:\n\n```yaml\n- name: Authenticate to GCP\n  uses: google-github-actions/auth@v2\n  with:\n    workload_identity_provider: ${{ secrets.WIF_PROVIDER }}\n    service_account: ${{ secrets.WIF_SA }}\n```\n\n| Property | Value |\n|----------|-------|\n| Token lifetime | ~1 hour |\n| Auto-refreshing | Yes |\n| Audit trail | Full via Cloud Audit Logs |\n\n### Build Secrets\n\nFor build-time secrets (never in image layers):\n\n```dockerfile\nRUN --mount=type=secret,id=npm_token \\\n    NPM_TOKEN=$(cat /run/secrets/npm_token) npm ci\n```\n\n```yaml\n- name: Build\n  run: |\n    docker build --secret id=npm_token,src=${{ secrets.NPM_TOKEN }} .\n```\n\n### Workload Repo Secrets (TF_VARS_)\n\nStatic secrets for deployed services are stored as GitHub Actions secrets on the **workload repo**, prefixed with `TF_VARS_`. These are passed to Terraform which creates Kubernetes secrets and GCP Secret Manager entries. See [Target Architecture — Secrets Management](target-architecture#secrets-management-in-workload-repos) for details.\n\n## Example Pipeline Run\n\n```\n1. Developer pushes to feature branch in code repo\n2. PR opened to develop or main\n3. Pipeline runs stages 1-4 (lint, secret scan, SCA, SAST)\n4. PR review and approval\n5. Merge to develop (for dev) or main (for qa)\n6. Pipeline runs all stages 1-7\n7. Image built, scanned, signed, pushed to Artifact Registry\n8. Developer updates image tag in workload repo values-{env}.yaml\n9. ArgoCD detects change and syncs to target namespace\n10. Service deployed and running\n```\n\n## Required Repository Configuration\n\n### Code Repo — Variables (Settings → Actions → Variables)\n\n| Variable | Example |\n|----------|---------|\n| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | `805102662749-...` (for Node.js builds) |\n\n### Code Repo — Secrets (Inherited from Org)\n\n| Secret | Description |\n|--------|-------------|\n| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` | Workload Identity pool provider |\n| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` | Service account for CI |\n\n### Workload Repo — Secrets (Per GitHub Environment)\n\n| Secret | Description |\n|--------|-------------|\n| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` | Workload Identity pool provider |\n| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` | Service account for Terraform |\n| `TF_VARS_*` | Per-environment static secrets for Terraform |\n\n### Branch → Environment Mapping\n\n| Branch | Environment | Namespace |\n|--------|-------------|-----------|\n| `develop` | dev | `dev-api-services` |\n| `main` | qa | `qa-api-services` |\n| `v*` tags | prod | `prod-api-services` |\n\n---\n\n**Next**: [GitOps & Promotion](gitops-promotion) — ArgoCD GitOps flow and environment promotion.\n"
}
```

   134→
   135→| Stage | On PR | On Merge | Purpose |
   136→|-------|-------|----------|---------|

   138→| 2. Secret Scan | ✓ | ✓ | Block secrets before commit |

   140→| 4. SAST | ✓ | ✓ | Static code analysis |

   144→
   145→:::note
   146→Stage 8 (GitOps update) from the previous centralized model has been replaced. Image tag updates are now made directly in the workload repo's `values-{env}.yaml` file, either manually or via automation.
   147→:::
   148→
   149→## Security Gates
   150→
   151→All gates are **fail-stop** — pipeline halts immediately on critical/high findings.
   152→
   153→### Gate Details
   154→
   155→| Gate | Tool | Stage | Trigger | Action on Failure |
   156→|------|------|-------|---------|-------------------|
   157→| **Secret Detection** | Trivy (`--scanners secret`) | 2 | Every PR + merge | Stop pipeline, no exceptions |
   158→| **Dependency Vulnerabilities** | Dependency-Track (via Syft SBOM) | 3 | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |
   159→| **SAST Quality Gate** | SonarQube | 4 | Every PR + merge | Stop on critical/high |
   160→| **Container Vulnerabilities** | Trivy (image scan) | 6 | Every merge | Stop on critical/high |
   161→| **Image Signature** | Cosign (Sigstore/OIDC) | 6 | Every merge | Unsigned images blocked |
   162→| **DAST Baseline** | OWASP ZAP | Post-deploy | Before prod promotion | Block promotion on findings |
   163→| **Admission Control** | Kyverno | Deploy-time | Every sync | Reject unsigned images |
   164→
   165→### Tool Configuration
   166→
   167→#### Trivy Secret Scan
   168→
   169→Scans source filesystem before any build:
   170→
   171→```yaml
   172→- name: Secret Scan
   173→  run: |
   174→    trivy fs --scanners secret --exit-code 1 .
   175→```
   176→
   177→Zero tolerance — any finding stops the pipeline.
   178→
   179→#### SonarQube
   180→
   181→Hosted in `shared-devsecops` namespace:
   182→
   183→- Runs static analysis on every PR
   184→- Quality gate: no new critical or high severity issues
   185→- Results posted as PR comment
   186→
   187→#### Dependency-Track
   188→
   189→SBOM-based vulnerability tracking:
   190→
   191→- Syft generates CycloneDX SBOMs
   192→- Uploaded to Dependency-Track for continuous monitoring
   193→- Pipeline stops on critical/high vulnerabilities
   194→
   195→#### Cosign Image Signing
   196→
   197→Keyless signing via GitHub OIDC → Sigstore:
   198→
   199→```yaml
   200→- name: Sign Image
   201→  run: |
   202→    cosign sign --yes $IMAGE_REF
   203→```
   204→
   205→No long-lived keys — fully automatic.
   206→
   207→### Vulnerability Remediation SLAs
   208→
   209→Per the Konecta DevSecOps Handbook:
   210→
   211→| Severity | Detection → Triage | Triage → Fix | Fix → Deploy |
   212→|----------|-------------------|--------------|--------------|
   213→| Critical | 4 hours | 24 hours | 48 hours |
   214→| High | 24 hours | 7 days | 14 days |
   215→| Medium | 7 days | 30 days | 45 days |
   216→| Low | 30 days | 90 days | Next release |
   217→
   218→## Image Tagging Strategy
   219→
   220→| Tag | When | Purpose |
   221→|-----|------|---------|
   222→| `sha-{7-char}` | Every build | Immutable build identifier |
   223→| `v{major}.{minor}.{patch}` | On git tag | Release version |
   224→| `{env}-latest` | On promotion | Environment pointer (mutable) |
   225→
   226→**Rule**: SHA tags are immutable. Never overwrite. Promotion copies the SHA tag reference, not the image.
   227→
   228→## Secrets Management (CI)
   229→
   230→### GitHub OIDC → GCP Workload Identity
   231→
   232→No JSON keys — fully automated authentication:
   233→
   234→```yaml
   235→- name: Authenticate to GCP
   236→  uses: google-github-actions/auth@v2
   237→  with:
   238→    workload_identity_provider: ${{ secrets.WIF_PROVIDER }}
   239→    service_account: ${{ secrets.WIF_SA }}
   240→```
   241→
   242→| Property | Value |
   243→|----------|-------|
   244→| Token lifetime | ~1 hour |
   245→| Auto-refreshing | Yes |
   246→| Audit trail | Full via Cloud Audit Logs |
   247→
   248→### Build Secrets
   249→
   250→For build-time secrets (never in image layers):
   251→
   252→```dockerfile
   253→RUN --mount=type=secret,id=npm_token \
   254→    NPM_TOKEN=$(cat /run/secrets/npm_token) npm ci
   255→```
   256→
   257→```yaml
   258→- name: Build
   259→  run: |
   260→    docker build --secret id=npm_token,src=${{ secrets.NPM_TOKEN }} .
   261→```
   262→
   263→### Workload Repo Secrets (TF_VARS_)
   264→
   265→Static secrets for deployed services are stored as GitHub Actions secrets on the **workload repo**, prefixed with `TF_VARS_`. These are passed to Terraform which creates Kubernetes secrets and GCP Secret Manager entries. See [Target Architecture — Secrets Management](target-architecture#secrets-management-in-workload-repos) for details.
   266→
   267→## Example Pipeline Run
   268→
   269→```
   270→1. Developer pushes to feature branch in code repo
   271→2. PR opened to develop or main
   272→3. Pipeline runs stages 1-4 (lint, secret scan, SCA, SAST)
   273→4. PR review and approval
   274→5. Merge to develop (for dev) or main (for qa)
   275→6. Pipeline runs all stages 1-7
   276→7. Image built, scanned, signed, pushed to Artifact Registry
   277→8. Developer updates image tag in workload repo values-{env}.yaml
   278→9. ArgoCD detects change and syncs to target namespace
   279→10. Service deployed and running
   280→```
   281→
   282→## Required Repository Configuration
   283→
   284→### Code Repo — Variables (Settings → Actions → Variables)
   285→
   286→| Variable | Example |
   287→|----------|---------|
   288→| `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | `805102662749-...` (for Node.js builds) |
   289→
   290→### Code Repo — Secrets (Inherited from Org)
   291→
   292→| Secret | Description |
   293→|--------|-------------|
   294→| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` | Workload Identity pool provider |
   295→| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` | Service account for CI |
   296→
   297→### Workload Repo — Secrets (Per GitHub Environment)
   298→
   299→| Secret | Description |
   300→|--------|-------------|
   301→| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` | Workload Identity pool provider |
   302→| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` | Service account for Terraform |
   303→| `TF_VARS_*` | Per-environment static secrets for Terraform |
   304→
   305→### Branch → Environment Mapping
   306→
   307→| Branch | Environment | Namespace |
   308→|--------|-------------|-----------|
   309→| `develop` | dev | `dev-api-services` |
   310→| `main` | qa | `qa-api-services` |
   311→| `v*` tags | prod | `prod-api-services` |
   312→
   313→---
   314→
   315→**Next**: [GitOps & Promotion](gitops-promotion) — ArgoCD GitOps flow and environment promotion.
   316→
```

  "content": "---\ntitle: GitOps & Promotion\nsidebar_label: GitOps & Promotion\nsidebar_position: 9\ndescription: ArgoCD GitOps flow, environment promotion, and rollback procedures\n---\n\n# GitOps & Promotion\n\nThis document covers the ArgoCD GitOps flow, environment promotion strategy, and rollback procedures.\n\n## GitOps Flow\n\n### Per-Workload Repository Model\n\nEach service has its own **workload repo** in `konecta-ix-workloads` that ArgoCD watches directly. There is no centralized GitOps repository for application deployments.\n\n```\nCODE REPO                              WORKLOAD REPO\nkonecta-ix-apps/                       konecta-ix-workloads/\nkix-app-iqportal                       kix-workload-iqportal\n────────────────                       ─────────────────────\n1. PR merged to develop\n2. Pipeline builds sha-abc1234\n3. Image pushed to Artifact Registry\n4. Developer or automation updates ──▶ 5. values-dev.yaml\n   image tag in workload repo              image.tag: sha-abc1234\n                                        6. ArgoCD detects change → syncs dev\n```\n\n### Workload Repository Structure\n\n```\nkix-workload-iqportal/\n├── helm/\n│   ├── Chart.yaml              # Depends on base-service-chart\n│   ├── values.yaml             # Service defaults (all envs)\n│   ├── values-dev.yaml         # Dev overrides (image tag, replicas, env vars)\n│   ├── values-qa.yaml          # QA overrides\n│   └── values-prod.yaml        # Prod overrides\n├── argocd/\n│   └── application.yaml        # ArgoCD Application manifest\n├── terraform/\n│   ├── main.tf                 # Service accounts, IAM, secrets\n│   ├── variables.tf\n│   └── backend.tf\n└── .github/workflows/\n    └── deploy.yaml             # Terraform validate-plan-apply\n```\n\n### Environment Values File\n\nEach environment has a values override file in the workload repo:\n\n```yaml\n# helm/values-dev.yaml\nbase-service-chart:\n  image:\n    tag: \"sha-abc1234\"  # Updated on each deployment\n\n  containers:\n    app:\n      env:\n        NODE_ENV: \"development\"\n        LOG_LEVEL: \"debug\"\n\n  autoscaling:\n    minReplicas: 1\n    maxReplicas: 2\n```\n\n## ArgoCD Configuration\n\n### Per-Workload ArgoCD Application\n\nEach workload repo contains its own ArgoCD Application manifest. ArgoCD watches the repo directly:\n\n```yaml\n# argocd/application.yaml\napiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1\nkind: Application\nmetadata:\n  name: kix-app-iqportal-dev\n  namespace: shared-argocd\nspec:\n  project: dev-project\n  source:\n    repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-iqportal\n    path: helm\n    targetRevision: main\n    helm:\n      valueFiles:\n        - values.yaml\n        - values-dev.yaml\n  destination:\n    server: https://kubernetes.default.svc\n    namespace: dev-api-services\n  syncPolicy:\n    automated:\n      prune: true\n      selfHeal: true\n    syncOptions:\n      - CreateNamespace=false\n```\n\n:::note\nMultiple ArgoCD Application manifests can exist in the same workload repo (one per environment), or environments can be managed via separate branches/tags. The example above shows the single-manifest approach with environment-specific value files.\n:::\n\n### Sync Policies by Environment\n\n| Environment | Sync Mode | When |\n|-------------|-----------|------|\n| **Dev** | `automated` (auto-sync) | 24/7 |\n| **QA** | `automated: false` (manual) | 08:00-18:00 UTC |\n| **Prod** | `automated: false` (manual) | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC |\n\n### ArgoCD Projects\n\nRBAC isolation per environment:\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1\nkind: AppProject\nmetadata:\n  name: dev-project\n  namespace: shared-argocd\nspec:\n  description: Development environment\n  sourceRepos:\n    - 'https://github.com/konecta-ix-workloads/*'\n    - 'https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/*'\n  destinations:\n    - namespace: 'dev-*'\n      server: https://kubernetes.default.svc\n  clusterResourceWhitelist:\n    - group: ''\n      kind: Namespace\n```\n\n## Environment Promotion\n\n### Promotion Workflow\n\nPromotion updates the image tag in the target environment's values file within the workload repo:\n\n```\nDev (values-dev.yaml)  →  QA (values-qa.yaml)  →  Prod (values-prod.yaml)\n  sha-abc1234                sha-abc1234               sha-abc1234\n```\n\n### Dev → QA Promotion\n\n1. Verify service is healthy in dev\n2. Update `helm/values-qa.yaml` with the image tag from dev:\n   ```bash\n   # In kix-workload-iqportal\n   # Update image.tag in values-qa.yaml to match values-dev.yaml\n   ```\n3. Create PR, review, merge\n4. ArgoCD syncs to QA (manual sync during change window)\n\n### QA → Prod Promotion (with DAST)\n\n1. Verify service is healthy in QA\n2. Run **OWASP ZAP baseline scan** against QA endpoint\n3. If ZAP finds vulnerabilities → promotion blocked, findings reported\n4. If ZAP passes → update `helm/values-prod.yaml` with the image tag\n5. Create PR with approval requirement\n6. After merge, ArgoCD syncs to prod (manual sync during change window)\n\n### Change Windows\n\n| Environment | Window | Notes |\n|-------------|--------|-------|\n| **Dev** | 24/7 | Auto-approve allowed |\n| **QA** | 08:00-18:00 UTC (Mon-Fri) | Manual approval required |\n| **Prod** | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC | Manual approval + change ticket |\n\n## Rollback Procedures\n\n### Manual Rollback via Git (Recommended)\n\nFastest rollback method — revert the image tag change in the workload repo:\n\n```bash\ncd kix-workload-iqportal\n\n# Revert the last commit (image tag update)\ngit revert HEAD\ngit push\n\n# ArgoCD auto-syncs (dev) or manual sync (qa/prod)\n```\n\n### Manual Rollback via kubectl\n\nFor emergency when git is unavailable:\n\n```bash\n# Roll back to previous ReplicaSet\nkubectl rollout undo deployment/kix-app-iqportal -n dev-api-services\n\n# Verify\nkubectl rollout status deployment/kix-app-iqportal -n dev-api-services\n```\n\n:::warning\nkubectl rollback doesn't update Git — reconcile Git state in the workload repo after emergency.\n:::\n\n### ArgoCD Rollback\n\nVia ArgoCD UI or CLI:\n\n```bash\n# List recent syncs\nargocd app history kix-app-iqportal-dev\n\n# Rollback to specific revision\nargocd app rollback kix-app-iqportal-dev <revision>\n```\n\n## Sync Waves\n\nResources deploy in order via sync waves:\n\n| Wave | Resources |\n|------|-----------|\n| 0 | Namespaces, RBAC, SecretStores |\n| 5 | ConfigMaps, Secrets |\n| 10 | Deployments, Services, HPA |\n| 15 | Ingress, NetworkPolicies |\n\nExample annotation:\n\n```yaml\nmetadata:\n  annotations:\n    argocd.argoproj.io/sync-wave: \"10\"\n```\n\n## Health Checks\n\nArgoCD monitors resource health:\n\n| Resource | Health Check |\n|----------|--------------|\n| Deployment | All replicas ready |\n| Service | Endpoints exist |\n| Ingress | Address assigned |\n| HPA | Current replicas within bounds |\n\n## Notifications\n\nArgoCD notifications on sync events:\n\n| Event | Channel | Recipients |\n|-------|---------|------------|\n| Sync success | Slack #deployments | Team channel |\n| Sync failure | Slack #deployments + PagerDuty | Team + On-call |\n| Health degraded | Slack #alerts | Team channel |\n| Prod deployment | Slack #prod-changes | Wider audience |\n\n### Example Notification Template\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: v1\nkind: ConfigMap\nmetadata:\n  name: argocd-notifications-cm\ndata:\n  template.app-sync-succeeded: |\n    message: |\n      Application {{.app.metadata.name}} sync succeeded.\n      Revision: {{.app.status.sync.revision}}\n      Environment: {{.app.spec.destination.namespace}}\n```\n\n## Disaster Recovery\n\n### Multi-Region Failover\n\n1. Secondary GKE cluster in eu-west4 (pre-provisioned)\n2. DNS-based failover to secondary cluster\n3. ArgoCD can deploy to secondary from same workload repo Git state\n\n### Recovery Steps\n\n1. Detect primary failure (health check timeout)\n2. Update DNS to point to secondary LB\n3. ArgoCD in secondary syncs from workload repos\n4. Services running on secondary within 15 minutes\n\n### State Recovery\n\nApplication state in managed services (Cloud SQL, GCS) — replicated separately.\n\n---\n\n**Next**: [Backstage & Onboarding](backstage-onboarding) — Developer portal and service onboarding.\n"
}
```

    10→This document covers the ArgoCD GitOps flow, environment promotion strategy, and rollback procedures.
    11→
    12→## GitOps Flow
    13→
    14→### Per-Workload Repository Model
    15→
    16→Each service has its own **workload repo** in `konecta-ix-workloads` that ArgoCD watches directly. There is no centralized GitOps repository for application deployments.
    17→
    18→```
    19→CODE REPO                              WORKLOAD REPO
    20→konecta-ix-apps/                       konecta-ix-workloads/
    21→kix-app-iqportal                       kix-workload-iqportal
    22→────────────────                       ─────────────────────
    23→1. PR merged to develop
    24→2. Pipeline builds sha-abc1234
    25→3. Image pushed to Artifact Registry
    26→4. Developer or automation updates ──▶ 5. values-dev.yaml
    27→   image tag in workload repo              image.tag: sha-abc1234
    28→                                        6. ArgoCD detects change → syncs dev
    29→```
    30→
    31→### Workload Repository Structure
    32→
    33→```
    34→kix-workload-iqportal/
    35→├── helm/
    36→│   ├── Chart.yaml              # Depends on base-service-chart
    37→│   ├── values.yaml             # Service defaults (all envs)
    38→│   ├── values-dev.yaml         # Dev overrides (image tag, replicas, env vars)

    43→├── terraform/

    45→│   ├── variables.tf
    46→│   └── backend.tf

    48→    └── deploy.yaml             # Terraform validate-plan-apply
    49→```
    50→
    51→### Environment Values File
    52→
    53→Each environment has a values override file in the workload repo:
    54→
    55→```yaml
    56→# helm/values-dev.yaml
    57→base-service-chart:
    58→  image:
    59→    tag: "sha-abc1234"  # Updated on each deployment
    60→
    61→  containers:
    62→    app:
    63→      env:
    64→        NODE_ENV: "development"
    65→        LOG_LEVEL: "debug"
    66→
    67→  autoscaling:
    68→    minReplicas: 1
    69→    maxReplicas: 2
    70→```
    71→
    72→## ArgoCD Configuration
    73→
    74→### Per-Workload ArgoCD Application
    75→
    76→Each workload repo contains its own ArgoCD Application manifest. ArgoCD watches the repo directly:
    77→
    78→```yaml
    79→# argocd/application.yaml
    80→apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
    81→kind: Application
    82→metadata:
    83→  name: kix-app-iqportal-dev
    84→  namespace: shared-argocd
    85→spec:
    86→  project: dev-project
    87→  source:
    88→    repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-iqportal
    89→    path: helm

    91→    helm:

    93→        - values.yaml

    95→  destination:
    96→    server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
    97→    namespace: dev-api-services
    98→  syncPolicy:
    99→    automated:
   100→      prune: true
   101→      selfHeal: true
   102→    syncOptions:
   103→      - CreateNamespace=false
   104→```
   105→
   106→:::note
   107→Multiple ArgoCD Application manifests can exist in the same workload repo (one per environment), or environments can be managed via separate branches/tags. The example above shows the single-manifest approach with environment-specific value files.
   108→:::
   109→
   110→### Sync Policies by Environment
   111→
   112→| Environment | Sync Mode | When |
   113→|-------------|-----------|------|
   114→| **Dev** | `automated` (auto-sync) | 24/7 |
   115→| **QA** | `automated: false` (manual) | 08:00-18:00 UTC |
   116→| **Prod** | `automated: false` (manual) | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC |
   117→
   118→### ArgoCD Projects
   119→
   120→RBAC isolation per environment:
   121→
   122→```yaml
   123→apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
   124→kind: AppProject
   125→metadata:
   126→  name: dev-project
   127→  namespace: shared-argocd
   128→spec:

   139→```
   140→
   141→## Environment Promotion
   142→
   143→### Promotion Workflow
   144→
   145→Promotion updates the image tag in the target environment's values file within the workload repo:
   146→
   147→```
   148→Dev (values-dev.yaml)  →  QA (values-qa.yaml)  →  Prod (values-prod.yaml)
   149→  sha-abc1234                sha-abc1234               sha-abc1234
   150→```
   151→
   152→### Dev → QA Promotion
   153→
   154→1. Verify service is healthy in dev
   155→2. Update `helm/values-qa.yaml` with the image tag from dev:
   156→   ```bash
   157→   # In kix-workload-iqportal
   158→   # Update image.tag in values-qa.yaml to match values-dev.yaml
   159→   ```
   160→3. Create PR, review, merge
   161→4. ArgoCD syncs to QA (manual sync during change window)
   162→
   163→### QA → Prod Promotion (with DAST)
   164→
   165→1. Verify service is healthy in QA
   166→2. Run **OWASP ZAP baseline scan** against QA endpoint
   167→3. If ZAP finds vulnerabilities → promotion blocked, findings reported
   168→4. If ZAP passes → update `helm/values-prod.yaml` with the image tag
   169→5. Create PR with approval requirement
   170→6. After merge, ArgoCD syncs to prod (manual sync during change window)
   171→
   172→### Change Windows
   173→
   174→| Environment | Window | Notes |
   175→|-------------|--------|-------|
   176→| **Dev** | 24/7 | Auto-approve allowed |
   177→| **QA** | 08:00-18:00 UTC (Mon-Fri) | Manual approval required |
   178→| **Prod** | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC | Manual approval + change ticket |
   179→
   180→## Rollback Procedures
   181→
   182→### Manual Rollback via Git (Recommended)
   183→
   184→Fastest rollback method — revert the image tag change in the workload repo:
   185→
   186→```bash
   187→cd kix-workload-iqportal
   188→
   189→# Revert the last commit (image tag update)
   190→git revert HEAD
   191→git push
   192→
   193→# ArgoCD auto-syncs (dev) or manual sync (qa/prod)
   194→```
   195→
   196→### Manual Rollback via kubectl
   197→
   198→For emergency when git is unavailable:
   199→
   200→```bash
   201→# Roll back to previous ReplicaSet
   202→kubectl rollout undo deployment/kix-app-iqportal -n dev-api-services
   203→
   204→# Verify
   205→kubectl rollout status deployment/kix-app-iqportal -n dev-api-services
   206→```
   207→
   208→:::warning
   209→kubectl rollback doesn't update Git — reconcile Git state in the workload repo after emergency.
   210→:::
   211→
   212→### ArgoCD Rollback
   213→
   214→Via ArgoCD UI or CLI:
   215→
   216→```bash
   217→# List recent syncs
   218→argocd app history kix-app-iqportal-dev
   219→
   220→# Rollback to specific revision
   221→argocd app rollback kix-app-iqportal-dev <revision>
   222→```
   223→
   224→## Sync Waves
   225→
   226→Resources deploy in order via sync waves:
   227→
   228→| Wave | Resources |
   229→|------|-----------|
   230→| 0 | Namespaces, RBAC, SecretStores |
   231→| 5 | ConfigMaps, Secrets |
   232→| 10 | Deployments, Services, HPA |
   233→| 15 | Ingress, NetworkPolicies |
   234→
   235→Example annotation:
   236→
   237→```yaml
   238→metadata:
   239→  annotations:
   240→    argocd.argoproj.io/sync-wave: "10"
   241→```
   242→
   243→## Health Checks
   244→
   245→ArgoCD monitors resource health:
   246→
   247→| Resource | Health Check |
   248→|----------|--------------|
   249→| Deployment | All replicas ready |
   250→| Service | Endpoints exist |
   251→| Ingress | Address assigned |
   252→| HPA | Current replicas within bounds |
   253→
   254→## Notifications
   255→
   256→ArgoCD notifications on sync events:
   257→
   258→| Event | Channel | Recipients |
   259→|-------|---------|------------|
   260→| Sync success | Slack #deployments | Team channel |
   261→| Sync failure | Slack #deployments + PagerDuty | Team + On-call |
   262→| Health degraded | Slack #alerts | Team channel |
   263→| Prod deployment | Slack #prod-changes | Wider audience |
   264→
   265→### Example Notification Template
   266→
   267→```yaml
   268→apiVersion: v1
   269→kind: ConfigMap
   270→metadata:
   271→  name: argocd-notifications-cm
   272→data:
   273→  template.app-sync-succeeded: |
   274→    message: |
   275→      Application {{.app.metadata.name}} sync succeeded.
   276→      Revision: {{.app.status.sync.revision}}
   277→      Environment: {{.app.spec.destination.namespace}}
   278→```
   279→
   280→## Disaster Recovery
   281→
   282→### Multi-Region Failover
   283→
   284→1. Secondary GKE cluster in eu-west4 (pre-provisioned)
   285→2. DNS-based failover to secondary cluster
   286→3. ArgoCD can deploy to secondary from same workload repo Git state
   287→
   288→### Recovery Steps
   289→
   290→1. Detect primary failure (health check timeout)
   291→2. Update DNS to point to secondary LB
   292→3. ArgoCD in secondary syncs from workload repos
   293→4. Services running on secondary within 15 minutes
   294→
   295→### State Recovery
   296→
   297→Application state in managed services (Cloud SQL, GCS) — replicated separately.
   298→
   299→---
   300→
   301→**Next**: [Backstage & Onboarding](backstage-onboarding) — Developer portal and service onboarding.
   302→
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs/docs/app-migration/kubernetes-helm.md",
  "old_string": "### Three-Layer Architecture\n\nThe platform uses a three-layer Helm architecture matching the multi-org, repo-per-service model:\n\n```\nLayer 1: Base Chart Library (platform org)\n         └── kix-platform-modules/helm/base-service-chart/\n             ├── Chart.yaml (type: library)\n             └── templates/_*.tpl\n\nLayer 2: Service Chart (each service repo)\n         └── kix-mcp-gateway/chart/\n             ├── Chart.yaml (depends on base chart)\n             └── values.yaml (service defaults)\n\nLayer 3: Environment Values (GitOps repo)\n         └── kix-platform-infrastructure/environments/\n             ├── dev/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml\n             ├── qa/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml\n             └── prod/kix-mcp-gateway.yaml\n```",
  "new_string": "### Two-Repo Architecture\n\nThe platform uses a layered Helm architecture with charts living in **workload repos** (not code repos):\n\n```\nLayer 1: Base Chart Library (platform org)\n         └── kix-platform-modules/helm/base-service-chart/\n             ├── Chart.yaml (type: library)\n             └── templates/_*.tpl\n\nLayer 2: Service Chart + Environment Values (workload repo)\n         └── konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-iqportal/helm/\n             ├── Chart.yaml (depends on base chart)\n             ├── values.yaml (service defaults)\n             ├── values-dev.yaml (dev overrides)\n             ├── values-qa.yaml (qa overrides)\n             └── values-prod.yaml (prod overrides)\n```\n\nCode repos (konecta-ix-apps, konecta-ix-mcp-servers) contain **only source code and Dockerfiles** — no Helm charts or deployment configuration."
}
```

  "old_string": "## ArgoCD ApplicationSet Strategy\n\n### Git Directory Generator\n\nArgoCD watches `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure`. Each file in `environments/{env}/` defines an application.\n\n| Environment | Sync Policy | Change Window |\n|-------------|-------------|---------------|\n| **Dev** | `automated` (auto-sync) | 24/7 |\n| **QA** | `automated: false` (manual) | 08:00-18:00 UTC |\n| **Prod** | `automated: false` (manual) | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC |\n\n### ArgoCD Projects\n\n- `dev-project` — RBAC for dev namespace deployments\n- `qa-project` — RBAC for qa namespace deployments\n- `prod-project` — RBAC for prod namespace deployments\n\n### Sync Waves\n\n- **Wave 0**: Infrastructure resources (namespaces, secrets, configmaps)\n- **Wave 10**: Application deployments",
  "new_string": "## ArgoCD Strategy\n\n### Per-Workload Application\n\nArgoCD watches each **workload repo** directly via an Application manifest stored in `argocd/application.yaml`. There is no centralized ApplicationSet — each workload repo is self-contained.\n\n| Environment | Sync Policy | Change Window |\n|-------------|-------------|---------------|\n| **Dev** | `automated` (auto-sync) | 24/7 |\n| **QA** | `automated: false` (manual) | 08:00-18:00 UTC |\n| **Prod** | `automated: false` (manual) | Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC |\n\nSee [GitOps & Promotion](gitops-promotion) for detailed ArgoCD configuration.\n\n### ArgoCD Projects\n\n- `dev-project` — RBAC for dev namespace deployments\n- `qa-project` — RBAC for qa namespace deployments\n- `prod-project` — RBAC for prod namespace deployments\n\n### Sync Waves\n\n- **Wave 0**: Infrastructure resources (namespaces, secrets, configmaps)\n- **Wave 10**: Application deployments"
}
