id: toolu_01MLpWNCJLZsvf1EGnP5T59F
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs/docs/app-migration/security.md",
  "old_string": "## Secrets Management\n\n### External Secrets Operator (ESO)\n\nESO syncs secrets from GCP Secret Manager to Kubernetes:\n\n```\nGCP Secret Manager → ESO SecretStore → ExternalSecret → K8s Secret → Pod\n```\n\n### SecretStore Configuration\n\nPer-namespace SecretStore authenticated via Workload Identity:\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1\nkind: SecretStore\nmetadata:\n  name: gcp-secret-store\n  namespace: dev-api-services\nspec:\n  provider:\n    gcpsm:\n      projectID: kd-ix-platform\n      auth:\n        workloadIdentity:\n          clusterLocation: europe-west1\n          clusterName: kd-ix-eur-dev-gke\n          serviceAccountRef:\n            name: external-secrets-sa\n```\n\n### ExternalSecret Example\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1\nkind: ExternalSecret\nmetadata:\n  name: kix-mcp-gateway-secrets\n  namespace: dev-mcp-servers\nspec:\n  refreshInterval: 1h\n  secretStoreRef:\n    name: gcp-secret-store\n    kind: SecretStore\n  target:\n    name: kix-mcp-gateway-secrets\n    creationPolicy: Owner\n  data:\n    - secretKey: DB_PASSWORD\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-mcp-gateway-db-password\n    - secretKey: API_KEY\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-mcp-gateway-api-key\n```\n\n### Secret Rotation\n\n- ESO refresh interval: 1 hour\n- Secrets auto-updated without pod restart (if using mounted secrets)\n- For environment variables: rolling restart via deployment annotation",
  "new_string": "## Secrets Management\n\n### Terraform-Managed Secrets\n\nSecrets are managed via **Terraform** in each workload repo (`konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-*/terraform/`). Terraform creates both GCP Secret Manager entries and Kubernetes secrets:\n\n```\nTerraform → GCP Secret Manager (audit trail) → K8s Secret → Pod\n```\n\n### Auto-Generated Secrets\n\nSecrets that can be generated (database passwords, HMAC keys) are created by Terraform:\n\n```hcl\nresource \"random_password\" \"db\" {\n  length  = 32\n  special = true\n}\n\nresource \"google_secret_manager_secret_version\" \"db_password\" {\n  secret=[REDACTED].db_password.id\n  secret_data = random_password.db.result\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"app_secrets\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"kix-mcp-gateway-secrets\"\n    namespace = var.namespace\n  }\n  data = {\n    DB_PASSWORD = random_password.db.result\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Static Secrets (TF_VARS\\_ Pattern)\n\nSecrets that must be provided externally (third-party API keys, OAuth credentials) are stored as **GitHub Actions secrets** on the workload repo, prefixed with `TF_VARS_`:\n\n```hcl\nvariable \"api_key\" {\n  type      = string\n  sensitive = true\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"app_secrets\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"kix-mcp-gateway-secrets\"\n    namespace = var.namespace\n  }\n  data = {\n    API_KEY     = var.api_key                # Static — from TF_VARS_API_KEY\n    DB_PASSWORD = random_password.db.result   # Auto-generated\n  }\n}\n```\n\nPer-environment secrets are defined using **GitHub Environments** (dev, qa, prod) on the workload repo. See [Target Architecture — Secrets Management](target-architecture#secrets-management-in-workload-repos) for details.\n\n### Secret Rotation\n\n- Re-run Terraform to rotate auto-generated secrets\n- Update GitHub Actions secret and re-run Terraform for static secrets\n- Rolling restart pods to pick up updated K8s secrets: `kubectl rollout restart deployment/{app} -n {namespace}`"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs/docs/app-migration/backstage-onboarding.md",
  "old_string": "### Option A: Via Backstage Software Template (Recommended)\n\nThe fastest way to create a new service:\n\n1. **Open Backstage** at `https://backstage.app.konecta.io`\n2. **Create Component** → Select template:\n   - Python API\n   - Node.js API\n   - Nuxt Frontend\n   - MCP Server\n3. **Fill in the form**:\n   - Service name\n   - Owning team\n   - GitHub org\n   - Cloud SQL requirement (yes/no)\n   - Ingress type (Kong/Gateway API/both)\n4. **Backstage scaffolds the repo** with:\n   - Standard `Dockerfile`\n   - Standard `chart/` directory\n   - Standard `.github/workflows/ci.yaml`\n   - Pre-filled `catalog-info.yaml`\n   - `docs/index.md` stub for TechDocs\n5. **Backstage creates GitOps entries**:\n   - `environments/{dev,qa,prod}/{service-name}.yaml` in kix-platform-infrastructure\n6. **ArgoCD auto-discovers** via ApplicationSet\n7. **First push** triggers standard pipeline\n8. **Service appears in Backstage catalog**",
  "new_string": "### Option A: Via Backstage Software Template (Recommended)\n\nThe fastest way to create a new service. Backstage scaffolds **two repositories** — one for code, one for hosting:\n\n1. **Open Backstage** at `https://backstage.app.konecta.io`\n2. **Create Component** → Select template:\n   - Python API\n   - Node.js API\n   - Nuxt Frontend\n   - MCP Server\n3. **Fill in the form**:\n   - Service name\n   - Owning team\n   - Code GitHub org (konecta-ix-apps, konecta-ix-mcp-servers, etc.)\n   - Cloud SQL requirement (yes/no)\n   - Ingress type (Kong/Gateway API/both)\n4. **Backstage scaffolds the code repo** (e.g. `konecta-ix-apps/kix-app-newservice`) with:\n   - Standard `Dockerfile`\n   - Standard `.github/workflows/ci.yaml`\n   - Pre-filled `catalog-info.yaml`\n   - `docs/index.md` stub for TechDocs\n5. **Backstage scaffolds the workload repo** (e.g. `konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-newservice`) with:\n   - `helm/` directory (Chart.yaml, values.yaml, values-{env}.yaml)\n   - `argocd/application.yaml` (ArgoCD Application manifest)\n   - `terraform/` directory (service account, IAM, secrets)\n   - `.github/workflows/deploy.yaml` (Terraform workflow)\n6. **ArgoCD watches the workload repo** and syncs the Helm release\n7. **First push to code repo** triggers standard CI pipeline\n8. **Service appears in Backstage catalog**"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs/docs/app-migration/backstage-onboarding.md",
  "old_string": "### Option B: Manual Onboarding\n\nFor cases where template doesn't fit:\n\n1. **Create repo** from `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template`:\n   ```bash\n   gh repo create konecta-ix-mcp-servers/kix-mcp-newservice \\\n     --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template \\\n     --private\n   ```\n\n2. **Copy application code** and configure:\n   - Update `Dockerfile`\n   - Configure `chart/values.yaml`\n   - Create `catalog-info.yaml`\n\n3. **Add to GitOps repo**:\n   ```bash\n   # In kix-platform-infrastructure\n   mkdir -p environments/{dev,qa,prod}/mcp-servers\n   touch environments/dev/mcp-servers/kix-mcp-newservice.yaml\n   # Configure values and commit\n   ```\n\n4. **ArgoCD auto-discovers** from git directory\n\n5. **Backstage auto-discovers** `catalog-info.yaml` within 30 minutes",
  "new_string": "### Option B: Manual Onboarding\n\nFor cases where template doesn't fit:\n\n1. **Create code repo** from template:\n   ```bash\n   gh repo create konecta-ix-mcp-servers/kix-mcp-newservice \\\n     --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template \\\n     --private\n   ```\n\n2. **Create workload repo** from template:\n   ```bash\n   gh repo create konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-mcp-newservice \\\n     --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-workload-template \\\n     --private\n   ```\n\n3. **Configure code repo**:\n   - Copy application source code\n   - Update `Dockerfile`\n   - Create `catalog-info.yaml`\n   - Configure `.github/workflows/ci.yaml`\n\n4. **Configure workload repo**:\n   - Update `helm/values.yaml` with service defaults\n   - Create `helm/values-{dev,qa,prod}.yaml` with env overrides\n   - Update `argocd/application.yaml` with correct repo URL and namespace\n   - Update `terraform/` with service account, IAM, and secrets\n   - Add `TF_VARS_*` secrets to GitHub repo settings per environment\n\n5. **ArgoCD picks up** the workload repo Application manifest\n\n6. **Backstage auto-discovers** `catalog-info.yaml` from code repo within 30 minutes"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs/docs/app-migration/backstage-onboarding.md",
  "old_string": "## Onboarding Checklist\n\n```markdown\n## New Service Onboarding Checklist\n\n### Repository Setup\n- [ ] Created from template or Backstage scaffolder\n- [ ] CODEOWNERS file configured\n- [ ] .dockerignore present\n- [ ] Branch protection enabled\n\n### Backstage Integration\n- [ ] catalog-info.yaml present and valid\n- [ ] docs/index.md created\n- [ ] mkdocs.yml configured\n- [ ] Service appears in Backstage catalog\n\n### CI/CD\n- [ ] ci.yaml calls standard pipeline\n- [ ] GitHub secrets/variables configured\n- [ ] First pipeline run successful\n\n### GitOps\n- [ ] Environment values files created\n- [ ] ArgoCD discovers the application\n- [ ] Dev deployment successful\n\n### Verification\n- [ ] Service running in dev\n- [ ] Health endpoints responding\n- [ ] Metrics visible in Grafana\n- [ ] TechDocs rendering in Backstage\n```",
  "new_string": "## Onboarding Checklist\n\n```markdown\n## New Service Onboarding Checklist\n\n### Code Repository Setup\n- [ ] Code repo created from template or Backstage scaffolder\n- [ ] CODEOWNERS file configured\n- [ ] .dockerignore present\n- [ ] Branch protection enabled\n- [ ] catalog-info.yaml present and valid\n- [ ] docs/index.md and mkdocs.yml created\n\n### Workload Repository Setup\n- [ ] Workload repo created from template or Backstage scaffolder\n- [ ] helm/Chart.yaml depends on base-service-chart\n- [ ] helm/values.yaml configured with service defaults\n- [ ] helm/values-{dev,qa,prod}.yaml created\n- [ ] argocd/application.yaml configured\n- [ ] terraform/ configured (service account, IAM, secrets)\n- [ ] TF_VARS_* secrets added to GitHub Environments\n- [ ] .github/workflows/deploy.yaml configured\n\n### CI/CD\n- [ ] Code repo ci.yaml calls standard pipeline\n- [ ] First CI pipeline run successful\n- [ ] Workload repo Terraform apply successful\n\n### Deployment\n- [ ] ArgoCD Application synced\n- [ ] Dev deployment successful\n- [ ] Service running in dev\n\n### Verification\n- [ ] Health endpoints responding\n- [ ] Metrics visible in Grafana\n- [ ] TechDocs rendering in Backstage\n- [ ] Service appears in Backstage catalog\n```"
}

{

  "old_string": "## Step 1: Create the New Repository\n\n### 1.1 Create repo from template\n\n```bash\ngh repo create konecta-ix-workloads/kix-usecase-iq-portal \\\n  --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template \\\n  --private\n```\n\n### 1.2 Clone and set up\n\n```bash\ngit clone git@github.com:konecta-ix-workloads/kix-usecase-iq-portal.git\ncd kix-usecase-iq-portal\n```\n\n### 1.3 Copy application code\n\nCopy the entire application source from the current `kon-iq-portal` repo. Final structure:\n\n```\nkix-usecase-iq-portal/\n├── backend/              # Express.js API\n├── src/                  # Nuxt 4 frontend\n├── public/               # Static assets\n├── scripts/              # Deployment scripts\n├── nuxt.config.ts\n├── package.json\n├── package-lock.json\n├── tsconfig*.json\n├── version.txt\n├── Dockerfile            # Modified (see Step 3)\n├── docker-entrypoint.sh\n├── .dockerignore\n├── catalog-info.yaml     # New (Backstage)\n├── chart/                # New (Helm chart)\n│   ├── Chart.yaml\n│   └── values.yaml\n├── docs/                 # New (TechDocs)\n│   └── index.md\n├── .github/workflows/    # New (CI)\n│   └── ci.yaml\n├── CODEOWNERS\n└── .env.example\n```",
  "new_string": "## Step 1: Create the Repositories\n\nIQ Portal follows the **two-repo pattern** — a code repo for source code and a workload repo for hosting data.\n\n### 1.1 Create the code repo\n\n```bash\ngh repo create konecta-ix-apps/kix-app-iqportal \\\n  --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-service-template \\\n  --private\n\ngit clone git@github.com:konecta-ix-apps/kix-app-iqportal.git\ncd kix-app-iqportal\n```\n\n### 1.2 Create the workload repo\n\n```bash\ngh repo create konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-iqportal \\\n  --template konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-workload-template \\\n  --private\n```\n\n### 1.3 Copy application code into the code repo\n\nCopy the application source from the current `kon-iq-portal` repo into the code repo:\n\n```\nkonecta-ix-apps/kix-app-iqportal/\n├── backend/              # Express.js API\n├── src/                  # Nuxt 4 frontend\n├── public/               # Static assets\n├── scripts/              # Deployment scripts\n├── nuxt.config.ts\n├── package.json\n├── package-lock.json\n├── tsconfig*.json\n├── version.txt\n├── Dockerfile            # Modified (see Step 3)\n├── docker-entrypoint.sh\n├── .dockerignore\n├── catalog-info.yaml     # Backstage descriptor\n├── docs/                 # TechDocs content\n│   └── index.md\n├── .github/workflows/    # CI pipeline\n│   └── ci.yaml\n├── CODEOWNERS\n└── .env.example\n```\n\nThe workload repo structure is configured separately (see Step 4 and Step 7):\n\n```\nkonecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-iqportal/\n├── helm/                 # Helm chart\n│   ├── Chart.yaml\n│   ├── values.yaml\n│   ├── values-dev.yaml\n│   ├── values-qa.yaml\n│   └── values-prod.yaml\n├── argocd/\n│   └── application.yaml  # ArgoCD Application manifest\n├── terraform/            # Service infrastructure\n│   ├── main.tf\n│   ├── variables.tf\n│   └── backend.tf\n└── .github/workflows/\n    └── deploy.yaml       # Terraform workflow\n```"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs/docs/app-migration/service-guides/iq-portal.md",
  "old_string": "## Step 5: GitHub Actions CI Workflow\n\nCreate `.github/workflows/ci.yaml`:\n\n```yaml\nname: CI Pipeline\n\non:\n  push:\n    branches: [main, develop, staging]\n  pull_request:\n    branches: [main]\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n  id-token: write\n  security-events: write\n\njobs:\n  pipeline:\n    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1\n    with:\n      service-name: kix-usecase-iq-portal\n      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile\n      chart-path: ./chart\n      node-version: \"22\"\n      build-args: |\n        VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${{ vars.VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID }}\n        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT=${{ vars.ENVIRONMENT }}\n        COMMIT_SHA=${{ github.sha }}\n      skip-tests: true\n    secrets: inherit\n```",
  "new_string": "## Step 5: GitHub Actions CI Workflow (Code Repo)\n\nCreate `.github/workflows/ci.yaml` in the **code repo** (`konecta-ix-apps/kix-app-iqportal`):\n\n```yaml\nname: CI Pipeline\n\non:\n  push:\n    branches: [develop, main]\n  pull_request:\n    branches: [main]\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n  id-token: write\n  security-events: write\n\njobs:\n  pipeline:\n    uses: konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-ci-workflows/.github/workflows/standard-pipeline.yaml@v1\n    with:\n      service-name: kix-app-iqportal\n      dockerfile-path: ./Dockerfile\n      node-version: \"22\"\n      build-args: |\n        VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${{ vars.VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID }}\n        NUXT_PUBLIC_ENVIRONMENT=${{ vars.ENVIRONMENT }}\n        COMMIT_SHA=${{ github.sha }}\n      skip-tests: true\n    secrets: inherit\n```\n\n:::note\nNo `chart-path` — Helm charts live in the workload repo, not the code repo.\n:::"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs/docs/app-migration/service-guides/iq-portal.md",
  "old_string": "## Step 6: Secrets Migration\n\n### 6.1 Create secrets in GCP Secret Manager\n\n```bash\nPROJECT=kd-ix-platform\n\n# For each secret:\necho -n \"value\" | gcloud secrets create kix-iq-portal-db-password \\\n  --project=$PROJECT --replication-policy=automatic --data-file=-\n```\n\n### 6.2 Create ExternalSecret\n\nAdd to `chart/templates/external-secret.yaml`:\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1\nkind: ExternalSecret\nmetadata:\n  name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets\nspec:\n  refreshInterval: 1h\n  secretStoreRef:\n    name: gcp-secret-store\n    kind: SecretStore\n  target:\n    name: kix-usecase-iq-portal-secrets\n    creationPolicy: Owner\n  data:\n    - secretKey: DB_PASSWORD\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-db-password\n    - secretKey: DB_USER\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-db-user\n    - secretKey: JWT_SECRET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-jwt-secret\n    - secretKey: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-google-client-id\n    - secretKey: RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-rag-pipeline-token\n    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-id\n    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-client-secret\n    - secretKey: CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-channelhub-connection-id\n    - secretKey: GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-image-bucket\n    - secretKey: GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-video-bucket\n    - secretKey: GCS_RAG_BUCKET\n      remoteRef:\n        key: kix-iq-portal-gcs-rag-bucket\n```",
  "new_string": "## Step 6: Secrets Migration (Workload Repo)\n\nSecrets are managed via **Terraform** in the workload repo (`kix-workload-iqportal/terraform/`).\n\n### 6.1 Auto-generated secrets (Terraform)\n\nAdd to `terraform/main.tf`:\n\n```hcl\n# Auto-generated secrets\nresource \"random_password\" \"db\" {\n  length  = 32\n  special = true\n}\n\nresource \"random_password\" \"jwt\" {\n  length  = 64\n  special = true\n}\n\nresource \"google_secret_manager_secret\" \"db_password\" {\n  secret_id = \"kix-iq-portal-db-password-${var.environment}\"\n  replication { auto {} }\n}\n\nresource \"google_secret_manager_secret_version\" \"db_password\" {\n  secret=[REDACTED].db_password.id\n  secret_data = random_password.db.result\n}\n```\n\n### 6.2 Static secrets (GitHub Actions → Terraform)\n\nAdd per-environment secrets to the **workload repo** GitHub settings:\n\n| GitHub Secret (per env) | Terraform Variable | Source |\n|-------------------------|-------------------|--------|\n| `TF_VARS_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | `var.google_client_id` | Google OAuth |\n| `TF_VARS_RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN` | `var.rag_pipeline_token` | RAG service |\n| `TF_VARS_CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID` | `var.channelhub_client_id` | ChannelHub |\n| `TF_VARS_CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` | `var.channelhub_client_secret` | ChannelHub |\n| `TF_VARS_CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID` | `var.channelhub_connection_id` | ChannelHub |\n\n### 6.3 Create Kubernetes secret (Terraform)\n\n```hcl\nresource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"app_secrets\" {\n  metadata {\n    name      = \"kix-app-iqportal-secrets\"\n    namespace = var.namespace\n  }\n  data = {\n    DB_PASSWORD               = random_password.db.result\n    DB_USER                   = var.db_user\n    JWT_SECRET                = random_password.jwt.result\n    GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID          = var.google_client_id\n    RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN        = var.rag_pipeline_token\n    CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID      = var.channelhub_client_id\n    CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET  = var.channelhub_client_secret\n    CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID  = var.channelhub_connection_id\n    GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET          = var.gcs_image_bucket\n    GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET          = var.gcs_video_bucket\n    GCS_RAG_BUCKET            = var.gcs_rag_bucket\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 6.4 Apply Terraform\n\n```bash\n# Run via GitHub Actions or manually\ncd kix-workload-iqportal/terraform\nterraform init\nterraform plan \\\n  -var=\"environment=dev\" \\\n  -var=\"google_client_id=$TF_VARS_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID\" \\\n  -var=\"rag_pipeline_token=$TF_VARS_RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN\" \\\n  # ... other TF_VARS_\nterraform apply\n```"
}
```

   224→| `{env}-latest` | On promotion | Environment pointer (mutable) |

283:           ├── QA: manual sync (08:00-18:00 UTC)
284:           └── Prod: manual sync (Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)
